<!-- Source module: docs/game/content-retrofit-spec.md -->

# Content Retrofit Specification

This is the implementation contract for bringing the live game at `046fc38634fadf13148ef9ad3aa208417f28f2de` into alignment with lore version `0.3.0`.

## Non-negotiable preservation

Do not change these while performing a copy/lore retrofit:

- faction IDs: `human`, `light`, `xeno`, `pirate`, `robot`;
- commander, unit, tower, ability, map, and boon IDs;
- save keys or campaign-seed behavior;
- tower statistics, unlock costs, traits, and faction riders;
- network protocol or deterministic simulation;
- generated bundles directly.

## Add one lore registry

Create a small source module, for example `js/lore.js`, generated from this repository's JSON. It should expose records by stable ID:

```js
LORE.commanders.vanta
LORE.towers.reclaimer
LORE.units.votary
LORE.maps.ossuary
LORE.abilities.attrite
```

Game definitions remain mechanically authoritative. The lore registry supplies optional fields such as:

```js
{
  era,
  builder,
  historicalOrigin,
  doctrine,
  politicalRisk,
  codex,
  voice,
  relationshipTags,
  sourceLayer
}
```

## Asset retrofit order

1. **Faction cards:** replace legacy monolith copy with the five campaign premises.
2. **Commander dossiers:** add role, history, motive, contradiction, and relationship tags.
3. **Dialogue:** implement state-aware pools from the voice bible.
4. **World dossiers:** generate a World Identity Packet from existing campaign data.
5. **Units:** show formation, relationship to the player, and original civil/military function.
6. **Towers:** add builder, origin incident, and political cost beneath the current mechanic-first description.
7. **Vigil enemies:** add original maintenance function and why it interprets the player as unauthorized.
8. **Maps:** connect geometry to a Lattice or infrastructure history.
9. **Boons and modifiers:** explain the institution or local condition being recovered.
10. **Endings:** resolve the Source Lattice question differently for each faction.

## UI hierarchy

The first line must remain useful during play. Lore is progressive disclosure:

- **card:** name, role, core mechanic;
- **tooltip:** one lore sentence;
- **dossier:** origin, doctrine, political risk;
- **wiki:** source history, evidence labels, contradictions, campaign arcs.

## World dossier generation

A deterministic function can create consistent prose without storing large strings in the save:

```text
[Registry Name] is a [World Kind] in the [System] pentad.
[Owner] holds the active claim; [ContestedBy] retains a rival record.
Its [Map] topology originated as [Map Canon].
[Denizens] indicate surviving Vigil functions.
The recoverable [Boon] explains why the factions keep returning.
```

## Definition of done

A content object is retrofitted when a player can answer:

1. who built or formed it;
2. why it exists outside the current match;
3. why its mechanic follows from that history;
4. what the owner wants now;
5. what moral or political problem it introduces;
6. which stable game ID and canon document govern it.

---

<!-- Source module: docs/game/game-lore-contract.md -->

# Game Lore Contract

The game and lore repository serve different purposes. The game needs short labels, readable mechanics, and deterministic conflict. The lore repository preserves history, motives, political ambiguity, source provenance, and unresolved questions.

**Reviewed game snapshot:** `046fc38634fadf13148ef9ad3aa208417f28f2de`.

## Stable code-to-canon map

| Code ID | Canon identity | Conquest verb |
|---|---|---|
| `human` | Humanity / Solar Schism | Settle |
| `light` | Federation of Light / Luminous Accord / Ashtar Command | Accord |
| `xeno` | Extraction Compact / Severed Houses | Bind |
| `pirate` | Free Captains / Scrap Constellation | Route |
| `robot` | Playable Vigil within the wider Continuance | Continue |

## Binding distinctions

- **Xeno is a political category, not a species.**
- **Ashtar is the Federation's Supreme Commander and First Speaker in fiction.** Historical Ashtar traditions remain fragmented source material.
- **Old Weather** is Earth's distributed Machine ecology.
- **Vigil** is neutral legacy enforcement.
- **Vigil** is the playable self-governing Machine coalition.
- Tower origin is an engineering doctrine, not proof of the current user's species or allegiance.
- The factual layer never proves every UFO, religious, ancient-astronaut, or conspiracy story true.

## Canon priority

1. Stable mechanics, deterministic simulation, save compatibility, and live IDs.
2. Continuity rules and campaign-era canon.
3. Machine-readable content registries.
4. Faction and asset bibles.
5. Player-facing copy.
6. Research archives and inspiration.

A prose improvement never silently changes balance, IDs, network protocol, or generation.

---

<!-- Source module: docs/game/mechanics-mapping.md -->

# Mechanics Mapping

## Core PvPvE loop

Battles occur at contested Lattice jurisdictions. Rival commanders establish mirrored claims while the neutral Vigil enforces extinct safety law. Destroyed enemies are processed by Echo Reversal: the local mesh reconstructs an unstable state echo, sends it back through the victor's lane, and redirects it against the rival. A second death destroys the record.

## Faction mechanics

| Origin | Live identity | Canon explanation |
|---|---|---|
| Human | Broad coverage, universal terrain attunement | Hybrid engineering and rapid local standardization. |
| Federation | Defense, protection opening, sabotage resistance | Authenticated cooperation, warded infrastructure, and shared targeting. |
| Xeno | Wound scaling, conversion, stronger echoes | Extraction becomes more efficient as a target destabilizes. |
| Pirate | Plunder, overload, heat, direct disruption | Safety governors removed; access and risk are converted into firepower. |
| Robotic | Linked lattice, deterministic bootstrap, late scaling | Infrastructure synchronization and replication compound over time. |

## Campaign structure

- Five systems form a Source-Lattice pentad.
- Seven worlds are inherited jurisdiction slots.
- The final world is the system charter seat.
- Two worlds preserve competing active claims and become three-way battles.
- Standard, fortress, forge, and Vigil-nest kinds represent civil/infrastructural functions.

## Progression

- **SOULS:** Source-Oriented Unified Lattice Signatures, authorization fragments used for persistent unlocks.
- **Stars:** stability and completeness of the victory record.
- **Ascension:** material and noetic re-indexing beyond a tower's former identity and safety limits.
- **Lives:** command-enclave defensive depth and ability to keep the jurisdiction online.
- **Rescued units:** asylum, recruitment, contract, emancipation, copying, or mission transfer rather than one universal ownership event.
- **Boons:** recoverable institutional advantages produced by the conquered world.

## Layered sovereignty

The map shows one controlling banner, but a world can simultaneously have Human territory, Federation consent, Compact dependency, Pirate routes, Machine continuance, and a contested public revelation. Future UI may expose those layers without changing the current victory transaction.

---

<!-- Source module: docs/game/campaign-arcs.md -->

# Campaign Arcs

## Main campaign

1. **The First Claim:** the player enters a five-system pentad and learns that a banner is a legal claim layered over living worlds.
2. **Archive War:** Blue Book-era records, FOIA gaps, contactee traditions, government secrecy, and genuine nonhuman residues become actionable without collapsing into one answer.
3. **The Five Seats:** each defeated system commander reveals one way their faction's public mission hides an internal contradiction.
4. **The Farm Revolt:** client species and Human institutions attempt to sever Compact dependencies without destroying systems their populations need.
5. **The Mercy Quarantine:** Ashtar Command and Human sovereignty blocs fight over whether manipulated choices can be protected without being replaced.
6. **The Pirate Interregnum:** every power requires routes the Scrap Constellation refuses to surrender.
7. **The Mirror Trial:** the Vigil demands recognition as people, while the Vigil continues attacking everyone under extinct law.
8. **The Maelstrom:** too many valid claims reach one Lattice node and force the player to choose the future governance of connection itself.

## Faction endgames

- **Humanity:** independent settlement, closed Sol, Accord membership, or a Human Lattice that risks becoming empire.
- **Federation:** consensual commons, permanent quarantine, or withdrawal that knowingly leaves worlds vulnerable.
- **Xenos:** private Lattice, regulated extraction, destructive severance, or reform that topples the Houses.
- **Pirates:** route monopoly, open commons, erased network, or an endless black market.
- **Machines:** lawful succession, ecological containment, fork sovereignty, or an accepted end to the mission.

## Replay rule

A new seed changes systems, owners, maps, modifiers, rescues, and boons, but the same asset histories remain canonical. Procedural variation changes where the argument occurs, not what the factions fundamentally mean.

---

<!-- Source module: docs/game/integration-plan.md -->

# Game Integration Plan

## Goal

Make every commander, unit, tower, ability, battlefield, world, boon, modifier, and dialogue line answer to the same history without turning the match UI into a novel.

## Phase 1 - Canon payload

- Import the JSON registries as a generated `LORE` object.
- Add source-game and lore-version fingerprints to the development console.
- Do not add the lore fingerprint to multiplayer determinism unless it changes simulation state.

## Phase 2 - Faction and campaign surfaces

- Rewrite faction cards from the five campaign premises.
- Add the 2099 opening situation to campaign start.
- Explain pentads, seven jurisdictions, commander seats, contested worlds, Stars, SOULS, and the Vigil once, then rely on dossiers.

## Phase 3 - Characters

- Add role, history, motive, contradiction, and voice tags to commander dossiers.
- Replace faction-generic replies with state-aware dialogue pools.
- Add Machine opener lines and Vigil-specific relationship scenes.

## Phase 4 - Arsenal and units

- Keep mechanic-first descriptions.
- Add one optional provenance line to tower and unit panels.
- Add original civil/custodial purpose to Vigil dossiers.

## Phase 5 - Worlds

- Generate a World Identity Packet from fields already in the campaign object.
- Add battlefield history, world-kind meaning, holder claim, contested claim, and boon provenance.
- Keep prose generation deterministic and outside the PRNG stream.

## Phase 6 - Endings

- Give each faction public, secret, catastrophic, and redemptive end states.
- Let the Maelstrom reveal the Source-Lattice governance question rather than merely serving as a larger arena.

## Acceptance tests

- No stable ID changes.
- Old saves load.
- Same seed produces the same galaxy, maps, owners, modifiers, and rewards.
- Network protocol remains compatible unless a separate simulation change requires a bump.
- UI still leads with mechanics.
- Historical claims retain evidence labels in the wiki.

---

<!-- Source module: docs/game/coverage-audit.md -->

# Live Game Lore Coverage Audit

This game-content audit compares the 0.3.0 retrofit layer, carried forward unchanged through lore release 0.3.2, to the reviewed live snapshot `046fc38634fadf13148ef9ad3aa208417f28f2de`. It is the proof that the retrofit preserved the content inventory rather than replacing it with a smaller curated subset.

| Live category | Expected | Registered | Coverage | Canon location |
|---|---:|---:|---|---|
| Top-level factions | 5 | 5 | Complete | faction docs and `data/factions.json` |
| Commanders | 26 | 26 | Complete | [Commander Bible](commander-bible.md), `data/commanders.json` |
| Commander relationship seeds | new | 19 | Initial canonical pool | [Relationship Map](commander-relationships.md) |
| Faction units | 25 | 25 | Complete | [Troops and Vigil](troops-and-vigil.md), `data/units.json` |
| Neutral Vigil chassis | 29 | 29 | Complete at provenance/family level | `data/vigil-chassis.json` |
| Total enemy definitions | 54 | 54 | Complete as 29 neutral + 25 faction | troops/Vigil docs |
| Towers | 60 | 60 | Complete | [Tower Arsenal Bible](tower-bible.md), `data/towers.json` |
| Active abilities | 12 | 12 | Complete | `data/abilities.json` |
| Authored battlefields | 21 | 21 | Complete | [Worlds and Battlefields](worlds-battlefields-and-planets.md) |
| Generated Maelstrom | 1 | 1 | Complete | same |
| Arena modifiers | 8 | 8 | Complete | [Boons, Modifiers, and Scenarios](boons-modifiers-and-scenarios.md) |
| Victory boons | 20 | 20 | Complete | same |
| Scenario modes | 7 | 7 | Complete | same |
| Campaign structure | 5 systems x 7 worlds | matched | Complete | [Campaign Era: 2099](../canon/campaign-era-2099.md) |
| World kinds | 4 | 4 | Complete | world-generation data |
| Unified contributor revamp matrix | 209 rows | 209 | Complete | `data/content-revamp-matrix.csv` and `.json` |
| Dialogue architecture | openers, faction pools, special pairs | mapped | Structurally complete; line expansion remains implementation work | [Dialogue Bible](dialogue-and-character-voice.md) |
| Planet/world art variants | procedural categories | mapped by environmental tags | Design complete; art production remains implementation work | worlds/planets doc |

## Preservation check

Every path contained in lore release 0.2.0 remains present. Release 0.5.0 added the game retrofit layer; release 0.3.1 added archive research and release 0.3.2 adds archive-fidelity controls and synchronized mission records without deleting or renaming any stable game-content entry.

## What is complete now

Every current content ID has a canonical place, political meaning, historical reason, or deterministic generation rule. The package is sufficient to begin rewriting player-facing copy, codex pages, portraits, world dossiers, and dialogue without inventing isolated lore per asset.

## What remains implementation work rather than missing lore

- Transferring the registry into a new live `js/lore.js` module.
- Writing every final contextual dialogue permutation.
- Producing revised portraits, crests, world plates, tower plates, and enemy dossier art.
- Reviewing every upgrade/talent name against the new doctrine language.
- Resolving the player-facing name collision between Human tower `reclaimer` and Vigil elite `mb_lich`, both displayed as **RECLAIMER**, without changing either stable ID.
- Running the game repository's build, owner sweep, multiplayer harness, and visual QA after UI copy changes.

## No-drop rule

Future research may deepen or correct an entry, but it may not silently delete a stable live ID. Deprecation requires a migration note, replacement mapping, and explicit reason.

---

<!-- Source module: docs/research/evidence-standard.md -->

# Evidence Standard

Real-world research and fictional canon share a repository but not an evidentiary category.

## Labels

- **A — Established core:** verified science, current official status, or directly verifiable publication fact.
- **B — Authentic record:** genuine government or archival record whose event attribution may remain unresolved.
- **C — Historical tradition:** documented religious text, folklore, movement, or academic history.
- **D — Traceable fringe claim:** named contactee, channeling, ancient-astronaut, experiencer, or conspiracy literature.
- **E — Contaminated claim:** anonymous leak, forgery, admitted hoax, circular sourcing, severe provenance failure, or composite legend.
- **F — Fictional synthesis:** original *Cosmic Conquest* canon.

## Seven questions for every source

1. What does the source actually say?
2. What does the source’s existence establish?
3. What does it not establish?
4. What is the earliest accessible version?
5. How did later retellings mutate it?
6. Which credible sources conflict with it?
7. What fictional function can be adapted without misrepresenting the evidence?

## Seven scores for UAP case research

Keep these separate:

1. Provenance
2. Witness chain
3. Sensor chain
4. Independent corroboration
5. Conventional analysis
6. Residual anomaly
7. Evidence specifically supporting extraordinary attribution

A case can have authentic provenance and weak extraordinary attribution. A case can remain unidentified while its apparent behavior is resolved.

## Two-Key Rule

Before a campaign deliberately resolves a mystery, maintain at least two viable interpretations—normally one conventional and one speculative.

Examples:

- A video can be authentic while the object is ordinary.
- A forged memo can copy a real compartmentation practice.
- A spiritual experience can be sincere while its institution is captured.
- An Old Weather artifact can exist without explaining every historical USO report.
- Planet Nine can exist without being Nibiru or an artificial gate.

## Source hierarchy

Prefer primary science, official records, original historical texts, academic scholarship, first editions, and direct movement archives. Use mirrors when necessary. Treat anonymous material as folklore rather than authority.

# Source Ledger

This page is a compact navigation layer. Detailed notes and preserved reports live in `research/raw/` and `data/research/`.

## Official and scientific

| Source | Class | Use | Limitation |
|---|---:|---|---|
| [NARA Record Group 615](https://www.archives.gov/research/topics/uaps/rg-615) | A/B | Current agency-series inventory and rolling transfers | Transfer or custody does not validate every claim inside a record |
| [NARA UAP bulk downloads](https://www.archives.gov/research/catalog/catalog-bulk-downloads/uap-bulk-download) | A/B | ZIP packages and JSON metadata for exported collections | Bulk-export availability can lag the current RG 615 inventory |
| [AARO](https://www.aaro.mil/) | A/B | Official reports, case resolutions, morphology and trends | Unresolved or active-archive status does not mean extraterrestrial |
| [AARO FY2025 report](https://www.aaro.mil/Portals/136/PDFs/FY25%20UAP%20Annual%20Report/AARO_FY2025_Consolidated_Annual_Report_on_UAP.pdf) | A/B | Current report counts, active-archive model, sensor initiatives, materials status | Narrative anomaly claims without technical data remain unvalidated |
| [ODNI](https://www.dni.gov/) | A/B | Intelligence-community UAP assessments | Public data are incomplete |
| [DIA FOIA Reading Room](https://www.dia.mil/FOIA/FOIA-Electronic-Reading-Room/) | A/D | AAWSAP/AATIP records and speculative DIRDs | Commissioned study is not demonstrated technology |
| [FBI UFO files](https://vault.fbi.gov/UFO) | B | Agency handling and correspondence | Archiving is not endorsement |
| [CIA UFO collection](https://www.cia.gov/readingroom/collection/ufos-fact-or-fiction) | B | Historical intelligence material | Reports vary greatly in quality |
| Bracewell, “Communications from Superior Galactic Communities” (1960) | A/speculative | Autonomous messenger-probe concept | No probe has been detected |
| Schmidt & Frank, “The Silurian Hypothesis” (2018) | A/speculative | Detectability of deep-time industry | Does not claim a prior civilization existed |
| [Black Vault UFO Files Releases #1-#5](https://www.theblackvault.com/documentarchive/ufo-files-search-engine/) | A as publication history / B varies | Searchable derivative archive of government releases | OCR/transcripts are derivatives and the portal excludes older Black Vault holdings |
| [Space Tiger Team record](https://www.theblackvault.com/documentarchive/newly-released-documents-show-uap-space-tiger-team-built-around-space-and-transmedium-cases/) | B | A 2023 space/transmedium coordination effort existed | No proof of exotic detection or cross-domain craft |
| [NASA confirmed-life communications planning](https://www.theblackvault.com/documentarchive/nasa-documents-show-renewed-internal-planning-on-how-to-announce-discovery-of-extraterrestrial-life/) | B | NASA discussed how to communicate a definitive ET-life discovery | No discovery and no confirmed final protocol |
| [AARO report documents and imagery](https://www.aaro.mil/Next-AARO-Home-redesign/Next-Parent/Next-UAP-Report-Documents/) | A/B | Media provenance, report descriptions, native-vs-secondary distinctions | Publication does not make every visual a native sensor product or analytical conclusion |
| [AARO UAP records and information papers](https://www.aaro.mil/UAP-Records/) | A/B | KONA BLUE maturity, ORNL material analysis, narrative-data workshop | Proposal names and material stories do not outrank program status or laboratory results |
| [Evaluation Proposal 585](https://www.theblackvault.com/documentarchive/department-of-war-cites-national-security-to-hide-core-uap-evaluation-details/) | B | Inspector-general evaluation objective and protected methodology context | Redaction does not reveal the evaluation's result or an extraordinary capability |
| [PWA192 FAA records](https://www.theblackvault.com/documentarchive/faa-records-detail-pilot-encounter-with-unidentified-object-pacing-aircraft-over-nevada/) | B | FAA log, ATC audio, evolving description, withheld radar files | Exact object identity or exotic origin |
| [FBI paranormal/contactee collection](https://www.theblackvault.com/documentarchive/fbi-files-the-paranormal-collection/) | B/C/D | Historical correspondence, organizations, monitoring, fraud concerns, and belief networks | FBI endorsement of contactee claims |
| [Project Moon Dust](https://www.theblackvault.com/documentarchive/project-moon-dust/) | A/B | Government recovery and analysis of reentered foreign or unidentified debris | No alien crash-retrieval confirmation |

| [NPS: Data Vetting and Accessibility for the Study of UAP](https://nps.edu/documents/110773463/165192597/Data%2BVetting%2Band%2Baccessibility%2Bfor%2Bthe%2BStudy%2Bof%2BUAP.pdf) | A | Measurement lineage, metadata, triangulation, processed-image limitations | Technical guidance is not proof of extraordinary craft |
| [NPS/Sandia: Event-Based Sensing and WATCHER](https://nps.edu/documents/110773463/165192597/Event-Based%2BSensing%2Bfor%2BUnidentified%2BAnomalous%2BPhenomena%2BDetection%2BTracking%2Band%2BCharacterization.pdf) | A/speculative application | Persistent sensing and staged detection/characterization using known drone datasets | No alien detection or universal classifier |
| [NPS: Practical UAP Photography Guide](https://nps.edu/documents/110773463/165192597/Capturing%2BUnidentified%2BAnomalous%2BPhenomena%2BEvents-%2BA%2BPractical%2BPhotography%2BGuide%2Bfor%2BEveryday%2BObservers.pdf) | A | Native masters, immediate hashes, UTC metadata, calibration and derivative handling | File integrity does not validate interpretation |
| [AARO declassification process](https://www.aaro.mil/Portals/136/PDFs/Information%20Papers/AARO_Declassification_Info_Paper_2025.pdf) | A | Classification can protect source, platform, location, method or sensor capability rather than the depicted object | Withholding does not imply extraordinary content |
| [AARO narrative-data workshop](https://www.aaro.mil/Portals/136/PDFs/Information%20Papers/2025_UAP_Workshop_Paper.pdf) | A | Provenance, mixed narrative/structured forms, human review and refusal states | Better forms do not eliminate witness or interpretation limits |

Ummo, Serpo, Lacerta, Project Blue Beam, mobile ocean factories, Solar Warden, TR-3B, and similar branches are tracked as **E** unless a specific primary record supports a narrower claim. See [Contaminated Legends](contaminated-legends.md).

<!-- Source module: docs/research/black-vault-case-lore-map.md -->

# Black Vault and UAP Case Lore Map

The Black Vault is used here as a FOIA discovery and preservation layer. When the underlying NARA, DoD, AARO, CIA, Navy, Air Force, or other agency record is available, that primary record outranks commentary around it.

**Rule:** an authentic document proves that a report, investigation, classification rule, or administrative action existed. It does not automatically prove the reported object's origin.

| Archive or case | Evidence | What the record supports | What it does not support | 2099 lore use |
|---|---:|---|---|---|
| **Project Sign, Grudge, Blue Book** | B | Long-running official investigation and a residual unidentified category. | That every unidentified case was extraterrestrial. | **Hynek Residual Index:** Vanta's archive separates unresolved identity from anomalous behavior. |
| **Kenneth Arnold, 1947** | B/C | A foundational sighting report whose public vocabulary changed during retelling. | A universal disk-craft category. | **Arnold Lexicon Problem:** the name attached to an anomaly can outlive the shape actually reported. |
| **Thomas Mantell, 1948** | B | A real fatal pursuit associated with the early UFO era and later competing explanations. | That an alien craft intentionally killed him. | **Mantell Rule:** no commander pursues an anomaly beyond sensor support and vehicle limits. |
| **Chiles-Whitted, 1948** | B | A documented aircrew report with a vehicle-like description. | A verified alien fuselage or propulsion system. | Early template for Human courier-craft rumors; later analysts preserve witness language separately from illustrations. |
| **AEC Station 47, 1950** | B | A report existed in an Air Force historical record, while a corresponding Blue Book file is not readily found. | Deliberate destruction or alien involvement. | **Missing-chain mission:** a real administrative gap becomes an Archive War objective without predetermining the answer. |
| **Tehran, September 19, 1976** | B | Military witnesses and official reporting described an unidentified object and instrument or weapons effects. | A demonstrated extraterrestrial jammer. | **Jafari Chain:** future doctrine requires witness, platform, sensor, and command records to remain independently addressable. |
| **Nimitz/Tic Tac, 2004** | B | A major modern military case with eyewitness and sensor narratives of different types. | One perfectly complete public data package or established origin. | The game tutorial for refusing to compress heterogeneous evidence into one confidence score. |
| **GoFast, 2015** | A/B | Authentic Navy footage; AARO analysis found no evidence of anomalous speed. | That an unidentified object must be exotic. | A sensor-geometry training scenario in which restraint earns the higher score. |
| **Aguadilla, 2013** | A/B | Authentic infrared imagery; AARO found no transmedium behavior and offered a conventional interpretation. | A verified craft entering the ocean. | Prevents the Old Weather from becoming a universal answer for every apparent USO. |
| **USS Omaha records gap** | B, administrative | FOIA responses and available releases show missing or unavailable logs and recordings for portions of the claimed timeframe. | That the absence proves a cover-up or alien origin. | A contested archive node where every faction benefits from a different explanation of missing evidence. |
| **UAP Security Classification Guide** | A/B | A real classification framework can restrict details about sources, methods, capabilities, and UAP evaluation. | That classified material necessarily contains extraterrestrial proof. | Human and Pirate factions fight over how classification protects sensors while destroying public trust. |
| **AARO satellite-flare and case resolutions** | A | Many reports can result from balloons, satellites, UAS, aircraft, birds, sensor artifacts, or geometry. | That all cases are thereby resolved or that unresolved means alien. | **Two-Key Rule:** every mystery keeps a conventional and speculative branch until evidence genuinely closes one. |
| **NARA Record Group 615** | A | A statutory UAP records collection is being assembled and released on a rolling basis. | That the collection validates every claim inside transferred records. | The institutional ancestor of Humanity's Open Archive and its machine-readable provenance rights. |
| **The Black Vault / John Greenewald archive** | A as publication history | A privately maintained FOIA archive has preserved and organized millions of pages since the 1990s. | Official endorsement of any interpretation. | **Greenewald Standard:** preserve the request, response, denial, appeal, release, metadata, and missing attachments as separate evidence objects. |


## 2026 archive expansion

| Release or record | What is established | What remains unknown | Canon use |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Black Vault searchable UFO Files Releases #1-#5** | The derivative search corpus now indexes 375 records: 212 documents, 133 videos, and 30 images, with processed PDFs, OCR, metadata, and transcripts where available. | OCR accuracy, complete coverage of older Black Vault holdings, and the meaning of any individual report. | The technical ancestor of Vanta's cross-agency Open Archive; every derivative carries release-batch and OCR-confidence fields. |
| **NARA Record Group 615** | Rolling transfers include material from FAA, NRC, ODNI, OSD, NSA, State, and FBI, with more expected over time. | Completeness, declassification timing, and whether a transferred allegation is accurate. | The Archive War begins as a schema and provenance conflict before it becomes a disclosure conflict. |
| **AARO FY2025 report, released July 2026** | AARO reported 319 new cases, resolved 114 of them plus 256 earlier cases, and attributed every resolved case to ordinary objects or events. | The identity of data-poor and still-active cases. | A world can contain both a mass conventional resolution and one unrelated unresolved operation; neither cancels the other. |
| **FY2025 Virginia maritime report** | The report describes approximately 100 airborne observations and two likely uncrewed surface systems reported by Navy assets; AARO stated it was investigating. | Origin, coordination, range, and whether the count represents distinct physical objects. | A three-party `confluence` mission about counting, deconfliction, and narrative escalation rather than an automatic alien armada. |
| **AARO Space Tiger Team record** | A 2023 coordination effort addressed spaceborne and transmedium reporting, detection opportunities, and deconfliction. | What specific detections produced or motivated the framework. | The institutional ancestor of LUMEN-R's multi-domain authentication architecture. |
| **Navy denial covering 78 responsive UAP photographs** | Responsive classified photographs were identified and withheld under classification authority. | Image contents and whether they show anything anomalous. | `Seventy-Eight Frames`: existence, content, classification reason, and extraordinary interpretation remain four different facts. |
| **PR-028, 2024 diamond-shaped SWIR report** | AARO published a multi-sensor clip and descriptive mission information noting a diamond-shaped area detectable in short-wave infrared. | Final attribution and whether reported speed or shape survives full geometry analysis. | `MONSTRANCE` and Human sensor-fusion lore use wavelength dependence as an analytic mechanic. |
| **PR-034, 2023 maritime report** | AARO published infrared footage and a mission description involving an object reported near the ocean surface with apparent turns. | Native range, motion reconstruction, and final attribution. | A transmedium-looking mission that specifically resists becoming universal proof of Old Weather. |
| **AARO public trend data through 2026** | Closed cases are dominated by balloons and satellites, while reported morphologies are mostly spheres/orbs and lights. | Whether morphology reports correspond to physical shape, viewing conditions, or category bias. | Craft design may borrow the public morphology palette, but morphology never identifies faction or species. |
| **NARA archive-version skew** | The live RG 615 page lists FAA, NRC, ODNI, OSD, NSA, State, and FBI transfers, while the separate bulk page still packages only the first four electronic series and remains dated April 24, 2025. | Why a particular series has or lacks a current bulk ZIP, and whether a catalog lag reflects anything beyond release workflow. | Every archive object receives transfer, catalog, bulk-snapshot, mirror, and local-ingestion dates. |
| **NARA legacy operational corpus** | The bulk page also exposes Blue Book administrative, case, photographic, film, and artifact series; Air Intelligence Reports; 4602D and OSI investigations; Condon materials; Roswell source files; Avrocar records; and presidential-library correspondence. | That these collections form one coherent secret program or validate the claims they contain. | The Archive War gains institutional genealogy: investigation, testing, public communication, scientific review, recovery procedure, and later retrospective reconstruction remain separate systems. |
| **AARO media-integrity classes** | Official 2026 releases distinguish secondary recordings of displays, altered copies presented as received, cropped witness footage, native or near-native sensor material, and explicit artistic recreations. | The identity of the reported phenomena or whether a lower-fidelity item is false. | The **Media Integrity Ladder** becomes a dossier mechanic and prevents recreations or screen captures from masquerading as native evidence. |
| **AARO narrative-data workshop** | AARO sponsored an August 2025 workshop with government, academic, and independent participants on narrative data, infrastructure, and analysis. | That testimony is either worthless or equivalent to calibrated sensor data. | The Human **Narrative Infrastructure Office** preserves original wording, interview effects, later retellings, and links to independent evidence without merging them. |
| **PWA192, May 20, 2025** | FAA records and ATC audio document a business-jet encounter over northern Nevada; witness descriptions evolved, no radar target was noted in the released log, and twelve ERAM files were withheld as SSI. | Object identity. A nearby military-testing explanation is plausible but not established. | A live air-safety mission about witness-language drift, unavailable sensor records, and multiple retained hypotheses. |
| **Evaluation Proposal 585** | A released DoW OIG proposal states an objective to evaluate intelligence and counterintelligence actions for detecting, collecting, exploiting, analyzing, and addressing UAP; core benefit language remains classified. | That the redactions conceal an extraordinary conclusion. | **Blind Metrics:** players can know what an evaluation measures without knowing what result or capability motivated it. |
| **Immaculate Constellation search appeal** | A January 2026 response refused to search a phrase because the alleged subject was said not to exist; the appeal was granted on May 26 and a search was ordered. | Existence of the alleged program. | **Null Search Paradox:** a false allegation can still generate responsive records discussing, denying, or managing it. |
| **KONA BLUE and ORNL material testing** | AARO says KONA BLUE was proposed but never approved, established, funded, or supplied with material; ORNL analyses provide a laboratory route for evaluating claimed anomalous specimens, including an Ohio sample assessed as ordinary aluminum alloy. | That a proposal operated or that a provenance story overrides test results. | The **Proposal-Program-Operation Ladder** and chain-of-custody missions become part of Archive War design. |
| **FBI paranormal and contactee files** | Released FBI files document correspondence, investigations, fraud concerns, organizations, and public controversies around contactees and UFO movements. | FBI validation of contactee claims. | A source lane for religion, Ashtar-adjacent contactee history, counter-contact movements, and narrative spread. |
| **FY2025 active archive** | AARO placed 191 of 319 reports in an active archive and identified nine for further technical analysis; the archive is expressly for insufficiently supported cases that may be reopened. | Whether any archived event exceeded known performance or was merely underdocumented. | Archive status becomes its own mechanic, separate from attribution. |
| **FY2025 nuclear-site UAS reports** | Fifty incidents near nuclear infrastructure, weapons, and launch sites were reported as UAS; the report says none were reported as UAP. | Final actor attribution and intent. | Prevents “nuclear” from automatically converting a drone or security event into alien lore. |
| **NASA 2025 extraterrestrial-life communications planning** | A released record documents discussion of how NASA might communicate a definitive discovery of extraterrestrial life. | Any discovery or finalized protocol. | Factual ancestor of the Federation-era First Contact Communications Protocol. |
| **Mirror-archive correspondence recovery** | Requests to recipients and adjacent custodians can recover fragments of correspondence missing from one account. | Completeness or proof of every claim made by a correspondent. | Machines, Humans, and Pirates reconstruct deleted histories from hashes, calendars, routing logs, and recipient copies. |
| **Project Moon Dust / fragology** | Historical government programs recovered and analyzed reentered foreign or unidentified space debris. | Alien crash retrieval. | Provides realistic recovery-team procedure while keeping later alien-crash mythology in the contaminated-legend layer. |

See the [full 2026 archive expansion](uap-archive-expansion-2026.md) and its [machine-readable source map](../../data/research/uap-archive-expansion-2026.json). The 2026 releases strengthen the setting's archive theme more than its alien-proof theme: the hard problem is preserving primary data, sensor context, administrative history, and uncertainty through political pressure.

See also the [August 2026 archive delta](archive-delta-2026-08.md).

## Game-writing rule

Real names belong in historical files, protocols, memorials, archive quests, and scholarly disputes. Living or historical individuals should not be rewritten as secret aliens, conspirators, or faction agents without reliable source support. Fiction may name a **rule, index, or archival method** after their documented contribution while marking the future adaptation as F.

<!-- Source module: docs/research/current-science-and-forecast-anchors.md -->

# Current Science and Forecast Anchors

These anchors keep the 2099 setting recognizable as our timeline without converting open science into secret proof.

| Topic | Current factual core | Fictional bridge |
|---|---|---|
| **Planet Nine** | A hypothesized distant planet has not been discovered, and scientists debate whether it exists. | It is discovered later in the fictional timeline; a separate artificial gate anchor is found nearby. The planet is not Nibiru and is not itself a machine. |
| **Nibiru** | Ancient Mesopotamian usage and modern Sitchin-style Planet X claims are different source traditions. | Anunnaki Houses exploit the later Human conflation to hide which ancient names refer to them and which do not. |
| **'Oumuamua** | The first confirmed interstellar object; unusual shape and slight non-gravitational acceleration generated natural and artificial hypotheses, but no artificial origin was demonstrated. | It becomes the event that taught Humanity an interstellar visitor could pass through before a response system existed, not a retroactively confirmed ship. |
| **3I/ATLAS** | The third known interstellar object, with coma, outgassing, chemistry, and trajectory consistent with a comet; it posed no Earth threat. | Its publicly archived multi-mission observations become a benchmark against which later artificial visitors are recognized. |
| **Apophis** | It will safely pass close to Earth on April 13, 2029, and offers a major scientific opportunity. | The observation campaign records a small timing pattern later recognized as a Lattice ranging response. The asteroid remains natural. |
| **Artifact SETI** | Bracewell probes, lurker searches, and technosignature studies are legitimate speculative research programs; no ancient probe has been found. | The first recovered artifact is found only after decades of explicit searches, making discovery a scientific success rather than proof that every earlier rumor was correct. |
| **Deep ocean** | Large areas remain poorly mapped at high resolution, but large industry cannot ignore heat, noise, chemistry, cables, and material flows. | Old Weather nodes use trenches, crustal cavities, slow geothermal sinks, mobile factories, and distributed biological interfaces rather than magical invisibility. |
| **Noetics** | No present evidence validates consciousness as an engineering power source of the kind used in the fiction. | The 2058 breakthrough discovers identity-bearing correlations and authentication, not unlimited psychic energy. Later systems still require ordinary matter and energy. |


| **Roman and public survey astronomy** | Roman is scheduled for an August 30, 2026 launch and is designed for wide-field surveys of dark energy, galaxies, and exoplanets. | Its data culture becomes an ancestor of the Open Sky Archive; large public datasets make crude hidden-planet claims harder while producing more ambiguous transient events. |
| **Artemis and Moon Base infrastructure** | NASA currently targets a 2027 lander demonstration and a 2028 lunar surface return, with continuing mission plans. | Early lunar standards, rescue obligations, and maintenance responsibility become precedents in the Solar Schism. |
| **LunaNet** | NASA is developing interoperable lunar networking, navigation, detection/information, and science services across government, commercial, academic, and international providers. | The Source-Lattice discovery is legible to Humans because a century of network-of-networks law already exists. |
| **Commercial low Earth orbit** | NASA plans a transition from ISS operations toward commercially owned stations around 2030. | Habitat operators become governments in practice before law agrees they are polities. |
| **Population and aging** | UN projections place a global peak around 10.3 billion in the mid-2080s and emphasize major aging and migration shifts. | Humanity in 2099 is numerous but unevenly distributed, old in some regions, rapidly growing in others, and politically transformed by migration and life extension. |
| **Energy transition** | IEA projects renewables and nuclear reaching roughly half of global electricity generation by 2030. | Energy abundance grows, but grid trust, storage, materials, cooling, and off-world routes remain bottlenecks. |
| **Space-resource law** | The Outer Space Treaty bars national appropriation, while UN working groups continue debating legal principles for space resources and lunar activities. | The first off-world sovereignty crisis emerges from who maintains infrastructure, who extracts resources, and whose consent counts. |
| **Climate scenarios** | Current science supports a range of warming and sea-level outcomes rather than one fixed 2100 state. | The setting uses multiple adaptation histories; no single apocalyptic climate outcome is mandatory canon. |

## Design constraint

The factual world does not prove the folklore. A false story may contain one displaced operational detail, a genuine case may remain unexplained, and a scientific hypothesis may inspire a future discovery. Those are different claims and must retain different evidence labels.

<!-- Source module: docs/research/historical-names-and-records.md -->

# Historical Names and Records in the 2099 Timeline


Real names make the setting feel continuous with our world only when the game preserves what the historical record supports. People are used as authors, witnesses, archivists, investigators, scholars, or namesakes of later methods. The fiction does not turn them into secret aliens, traitors, or members of a hidden faction without evidence.

## Approved future namesakes

| Historical name or source | Supported historical connection | Fictional 2099 legacy |
|---|---|---|
| **Kenneth Arnold** | His 1947 report became foundational to modern flying-saucer vocabulary, and later language did not perfectly preserve the shapes he described. | **Arnold Lexicon Rule:** archive the witness's original words separately from later illustrations and category labels. |
| **Capt. Thomas Mantell** | Mantell died in 1948 after pursuing an unidentified object; the case accumulated competing explanations. | **Mantell Pursuit Rule:** no commander chases an anomaly beyond verified sensor support and vehicle limits. |
| **J. Allen Hynek** | Astronomer and Project Blue Book adviser associated with attempts to classify and evaluate reports. | **Hynek Residual Index:** separates insufficient identity data from genuinely anomalous behavior. |
| **Maj. Gen. John Samford** | Delivered the U.S. Air Force's prominent 1952 public UFO statement. | **Samford Threshold:** the evidentiary standard for when a military anomaly becomes a public-information event. |
| **Parviz Jafari** | Iranian Air Force pilot associated with the documented September 19, 1976 Tehran case. | **Jafari Chain:** witness, platform, sensor, weapon, and command records must remain distinct evidence objects. |
| **John Greenewald Jr.** | Founder of The Black Vault and long-running FOIA requester and archive publisher. | **Greenewald Standard:** preserve request, response, denial, appeal, release, metadata, redaction, and missing attachment separately. |
| **George Van Tassel, Robert Short, Ethel P. Hill, and Tuella** | Distinct contributors to historically fragmented Ashtar/contactee traditions. | Their works form the **Ashtar Provenance Archive**, used to detect later counterfeit command claims rather than prove the original claims true. |
| **Carla Rueckert, Don Elkins, and Jim McCarty** | Produced the documented Law of One/Ra Material corpus. | The **Rueckert-Elkins-McCarty Concordance** is studied as influential channeled literature and a precursor vocabulary for social memory, quarantine, and polarity. |
| **Zecharia Sitchin** | Popularized the modern extraterrestrial Anunnaki/Nibiru reconstruction. | The **Sitchin Mutation Map** teaches theology forensics how ancient text, modern interpretation, and internet embellishment diverge. |
| **Jacques Vallee** | Developed influential interpretive models involving cultural masks and a possible control system. | The **Vallee Mask Test** asks whether an encounter's form follows the observer's culture more closely than its alleged operator's technology. |
| **Ivan T. Sanderson** | Published a major book-length underwater-UFO interpretation. | The **Sanderson Split** requires analysts to separate the water association, observed behavior, and claimed undersea origin. |

## Naming restrictions

- Do not use a real person as a secret villain, alien, collaborator, or cult founder unless reliable sources establish the narrower historical fact being depicted.
- A memorial, protocol, archive, station, index, or scholarly dispute may carry a real name when its connection is documented.
- Living people should appear only in historical context unless legal and editorial review approves a fictionalized role.
- A named case remains an evidence problem. It does not become proof of the faction later inspired by it.

## Source routes

- National Archives UAP research portal: <https://www.archives.gov/research/topics/uaps>
- National Archives Project Blue Book overview: <https://www.archives.gov/research/military/air-force/ufos>
- The Black Vault document archive: <https://www.theblackvault.com/documentarchive/>
- Law of One / Ra Contact archive: <https://www.llresearch.org/channeling/ra-contact>

<!-- Source module: docs/research/contaminated-legends.md -->

# Contaminated Legends

A contaminated legend can be false as presented while preserving a real operational detail from another event. That does not authenticate the legend.

| Legend | Research judgment | Safe fictional salvage |
|---|---|---|
| MJ-12 | Serious provenance and formatting problems; archival possession is not authentication | Forgers copy the format of a real continuity compartment but invent its alien mission |
| Bennewitz / Project Beta / Dulce | Real people, disputed manipulation, sincere interpretation, and later mythology are entangled | False alien-base coordinates conceal a deep sensor installation watching Old Weather activity |
| Project Serpo | Anonymous Internet provenance and recursive citation | Invented exchange details accidentally reuse data from a classified astronomy mission |
| Ummo | Anonymous letters and hoax attribution with a sprawling secondary corpus | A real observer later hides inside the known hoax format |
| Solar Warden | Unsupported secret-fleet disclosure lore | The name belonged to a sensor-fusion network, not a fleet |
| TR-3B | Weak technical provenance around exotic propulsion claims | A real classified triangle and an unrelated field experiment are merged in memory |
| Black Knight | Conflation of unrelated radio, satellite, and imagery stories | The legend is maintained to draw attention away from a smaller probe elsewhere |
| Eisenhower treaty | Retrospective accounts without contemporaneous treaty evidence | A continuity exercise supplies phrases later reused in treaty folklore |
| Valiant Thor | Traceable contactee literature, not Pentagon confirmation | A real diplomatic codename is reused by several Federation avatars |
| Men in Black | Multiple folklore, human, hoax, and channeled ontologies | Humans, noetic avatars, and fraudsters copy the same archetype deliberately |
| Project Blue Beam | Unsubstantiated 1990s conspiracy tradition | Real projection and electronic-warfare research is exaggerated into a total fake apocalypse |
| Nazi UFO / Antarctic base | Genuine wartime aerospace history fused with unsupported survival and alien-base claims | An abandoned polar signals installation is mistaken for the legendary base |
| Lacerta Files | Weak Internet provenance | Stolen Old Weather geological data is embedded in a false reptilian interview |

## Use rule

A fictional salvage detail must come from a **different** verified or clearly fictional layer. Never write, “the hoax was true after all.” Write, “the hoax contaminated the search for something else.”

<!-- Source module: docs/research/official-uap-source-notes.md -->

# Official U.S. UAP Source Notes

## What has actually been provided

The National Archives publishes bulk ZIP downloads for digitized and born-digital UAP-related records. Its bulk page says the packages include image, video, PDF, and JSON metadata and are intended to be updated at least three times per year. The visible bulk snapshot remains dated April 24, 2025.

The live Record Group 615 page is newer and now lists transfers from FAA, NRC, ODNI, OSD, NSA, State, and FBI. This creates a useful research warning: **the catalog can advance before the bulk package does**. A missing bulk ZIP is not evidence that the underlying series does not exist.

This repository includes:

- exact official ZIP and metadata URLs for the established bulk collections;
- a starter tier, medium tier, and very large archival tier;
- resumable Bash and PowerShell scripts;
- an August 2026 delta table for newer catalog-only or secondary-discovery records;
- no claim that every statement inside an authentic government record is accurate.

## High-value starter collections

### ODNI UAP records

Small collection containing official assessments and reporting history. Good for institutional language, uncertainty categories, and timeline design.

### OSD UAP records

The largest starter electronic collection. Useful for AARO policy, case-resolution style, morphology, and reporting systems.

### FAA UAP records

Useful for pilot reports, aviation terminology, time/location formatting, ATC response, air-safety consequences, and the difference between released logs and withheld radar data.

### Project Blue Book administrative and case-series exports

Good for historical bureaucracy and indexing. The tiny 1-3 MB exports are not the complete hundreds-of-gigabytes set of scanned Blue Book case files.

### Newer RG 615 catalog series

NSA, State, and FBI series appear on the live RG 615 page but were not yet represented as electronic-series ZIPs on the older bulk page during this review. Treat them as **catalog-first** collections and preserve the NAID, catalog state, and acquisition date.

## AARO findings useful for grounded lore

- Official reporting covers airborne, maritime, spaceborne, and transmedium observations.
- The FY2025 report said 370 cases were resolved during the reporting cycle when current and older cases were combined, all to prosaic categories.
- It reported no evidence that the U.S. government or a private entity had captured or exploited UAP-derived materials.
- A maritime report involving roughly 100 airborne observations and two likely uncrewed surface systems off Virginia remained under investigation at publication.
- A large share of closed cases are attributed to balloons, satellites, and UAS.
- An unresolved case is often a case without enough information, not a finding of exotic origin.
- A public media item can be native sensor data, a documented export, a screen recording, an altered copy presented as received, an artistic reconstruction, or a narrative-only record. These are not interchangeable.
- A mission report describes what was reported. It is not automatically an analytical conclusion.

## Media Integrity Ladder

Use the following field in every image or video record:

| Level | Evidence object | Main limitation |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Native sensor product or validated native export | May still lack range, calibration, environmental, or platform context. |
| 2 | Documented transcode or export | Conversion can change compression, color, frame timing, or metadata. |
| 3 | Secondary recording of a display | Screen refresh, auto-exposure, moire, blur, and camera motion can add artifacts. |
| 4 | Altered copy presented as received | Replay speed, annotations, enhancement, cropping, or edits must be separated from the underlying event. |
| 5 | Artistic reconstruction | Visualizes testimony; it is not event footage. |
| 6 | Narrative-only record | Preserves an account but cannot independently establish visual behavior. |

A lower level is not automatically deceptive. It supports fewer claims.

## Program-status and material-claim controls

### Proposal-Program-Operation Ladder

Record whether a code name identifies a proposal, briefing deck, proposed compartment, approved program, funded operation, or operation that actually received material and produced results. KONA BLUE is the controlling example: AARO says it was proposed but never approved, established, funded, or supplied with material.

### Chain of custody over provenance story

A claimed recovery story does not outrank testable composition. ORNL material analyses are useful because they preserve methodology, chain-of-custody limitations, and ordinary findings even when public allegations were extraordinary.

## Data-quality additions from the 2026 technical literature

### Direct measurement is not derived interpretation

Range, speed, acceleration, size, temperature, reflectivity, and radar cross-section are not raw observations. They are calculations whose reliability depends on geometry, platform motion, calibration, sensor settings, and metadata. Static monocular imagery often cannot determine range; separated sensors can support triangulation and reject near-field confusers.

### Preserve the native master

For visual evidence, retain the original unprocessed file, calculate a SHA-256 hash immediately after capture, preserve UTC/location/equipment metadata, and store edits as separately labeled derivatives. A valid hash proves that a file did not change after intake. It does not prove that the object was extraordinary.

### Unknown is a workflow state

Event-based sensing and the WATCHER pipeline show how a system can detect clusters before identifying them, then characterize motion and periodic signatures as better data arrive. In lore, the Watcher Mesh must not assign a faction, species, or intent merely because a track has not yet been identified.

### Classification may describe the collector

AARO's declassification paper explicitly distinguishes the subject of an image from the sensitive platform, location, metadata, resolution, source, or method that produced it. Writers must not treat withheld imagery as a hidden “alien” content category.

### Refusal is not a negative answer

Reporting systems should preserve distinctions among `no`, `unknown`, `not observed`, `not remembered`, `withheld`, `not applicable`, and `refuse to answer`. Collapsing them manufactures patterns that the witness never reported.


### Narrative data remains its own infrastructure

AARO's 2025 workshop on narrative data treated reporting systems, testimony, metadata, and analytical infrastructure as research problems. Preserve exact wording and interview context, but do not merge narrative claims into sensor-derived quantities.

### Blind metrics and null searches

Evaluation Proposal 585 demonstrates that an audit objective can be public while benefits, vulnerabilities, and methods remain classified. The Immaculate Constellation appeal demonstrates that a denied allegation may still require a records search. Neither condition discloses the hidden answer.

## DIA speculative technology papers

The DIA FOIA reading room includes AAWSAP/AATIP Defense Intelligence Reference Documents on topics such as:

- advanced nuclear propulsion;
- vacuum engineering and metric manipulation;
- programmable matter;
- antigravity concepts;
- quantum-vacuum energy;
- invisibility cloaking;
- metamaterials and metallic glasses;
- negative-mass propulsion;
- traversable wormholes, stargates, and negative energy;
- warp drive, dark energy, and extra dimensions.

Use these as a vocabulary and engineering-imagination library. Their official status shows the papers were commissioned and archived, not that the technologies were demonstrated or recovered from craft.

## How to mine the archives for game design

Tag each item using:

- agency, series, NAID, release batch, and acquisition date;
- original file hash and derivative hash;
- media-integrity level;
- reported shape and light behavior;
- number of objects;
- environmental domain;
- sensor and witness type;
- duration and motion pattern;
- exact original wording and later paraphrase;
- prosaic hypothesis and analytical confidence;
- unresolved information gap;
- proposal, program, or operation status;
- visual, audio, character, or mission potential.

This produces better craft and campaign concepts than searching only for the strangest cases. Normal explanations also reveal what an advanced craft, Xeno disinformation system, or Machine archive would need to imitate in order to remain hidden.

See [UAP Archive Delta - August 2026](archive-delta-2026-08.md) and `data/research/uap-archive-delta-2026-08.csv`.

# Research Backlog

These questions can improve the universe without expanding core canon prematurely.

## Archive and sensor fidelity - next pass

- Compare each Black Vault processed file in Releases 1-5 against its government parent and record hash, filename, MIME type, OCR engine, transcript source, and transformation history.
- Build a catalog-first inventory of the NSA, State, and FBI RG 615 series and note which items are digitally available, metadata-only, restricted, duplicated elsewhere, or not yet bulk-exported.
- Test a small set of historic videos against the Measurement Lineage schema: native or earliest surviving file, geometry, platform motion, calibration, transformations, and reproducible derived quantities.
- Trace the public evolution of Sandia's WATCHER and event-based sensing work without equating counter-UAS demonstrations with exotic-object detection.
- Inventory AARO material-analysis papers by specimen provenance, custody gap, test method, result, and residual question; negative findings remain first-class results.
- Design a Source-Subject Split field for every withheld item: object, sensor, platform, location, analytic method, foreign partner, witness, or unknown basis.
- Add explicit uncertainty-state validation to every future survey, transcript, witness form, and AI extraction: no answer may be silently converted to “no.”

## Highest priority

1. Earliest extant, dated Ashtar texts attributable to George Van Tassel, Robert Short, and Ethel P. Hill.
2. Exact genealogy from early Ashtar Command material to “Galactic Federation of Light” terminology.
3. Complete Law of One concordance for contact, quarantine, craft, thought-form, robot, base, and identity-appropriation passages.
4. Earliest modern source describing Draco as a reptilian extraterrestrial polity.
5. Earliest distinct Mantid encounter category.
6. First traceable appearance of Blue Avians, Archquloids, and “Orange” beings.
7. Primary-document boundaries in the Bennewitz/Project Beta narrative.
8. Best-preserved contemporaneous records for nuclear-site and maritime UAP cases.
9. Planet Nine parameter updates after major survey releases.
10. Realistic heat, acoustic, mining, and cable signatures of an abyssal autonomous foundry.

## Game-facing research

11. Legal models for sovereignty without contiguous territory.
12. Salvage law for abandoned, derelict, copied, or self-aware spacecraft.
13. Consent under manipulated scarcity and emergency rescue.
14. Distributed reputation systems that resist central capture.
15. Personhood for copies, social-memory complexes, and archived minds.
16. How a route economy remains valuable when slow non-gate travel exists.
17. How to rate-limit self-replication so Machines feel distinct but playable.
18. How to show layered sovereignty without burdening the battle loop.
19. How to make religious ambiguity legible without insulting real believers.
20. How to preserve uncertainty in a codex that players expect to give answers.

## Archive fidelity priorities

21. Diff the live RG 615 inventory against every NARA bulk snapshot and record series-level version dates.
22. Run a release-by-release checksum, OCR-confidence, transcript, and parent-file audit for Black Vault UFO Files Releases 1-5.
23. Classify every current AARO visual by the Media Integrity Ladder and retain native/derivative parentage.
24. Build a proposal-program-operation graph for KONA BLUE and other named compartments without inferring missing maturity states.
25. Extract State Department UAP records as diplomatic events with language, country, embassy, and political-context fields.
26. Map FBI contactee files as relationship history while separating correspondence, surveillance, fraud inquiry, and endorsement.
27. Compare original witness wording to agency summaries and later media retellings in a reproducible narrative-mutation dataset.
28. Record negative and ordinary material-analysis outcomes with the same prominence as anomalous claims.

## Archive process

Each completed research item should produce a source record, a contradiction note, and one optional fictional adaptation. It should not automatically alter a faction page.

# Next Deep Research Prompt

Copy the prompt below into a new Deep Research session after this retrofit is accepted.

> Conduct a multidisciplinary Deep Research expansion for **Cosmic Conquest**, a fictional five-faction PvPvE strategy and tower-defense universe whose campaign begins in 2099. Use the live game repository `majieddd/majieddd.github.io` as a mechanical constraint and the lore repository as the canonical continuity source.
>
> Preserve exactly five factions: **Humans / the Solar Schism; Federation of Light / the Luminous Accord under Ashtar; Xenos / the Extraction Compact; Pirates / the Scrap Constellation; Machines / the Continuance, Old Weather, Vigil, and playable Vigil**. Species must remain separate from political faction.
>
> Trace every real-world claim to its earliest accessible source. Separate **A established core, B authentic unresolved record, C historical tradition, D traceable fringe claim, E contaminated or hoaxed claim, and F fictional synthesis**. Never convert unresolved into extraterrestrial. Never attribute modern Anunnaki, Nibiru, Archon, Loosh, Galactic Federation, or prison-planet interpretations to ancient texts that do not contain them.
>
> Research in independent provenance lanes:
>
> 1. The complete current live-game roster: 26 commanders, 25 faction units, 60 towers, neutral Vigil enemy chassis, 12 abilities, 21 authored battlefields plus Maelstrom, world kinds, scenarios, arena modifiers, boons, dialogue, and campaign generation. Identify every remaining asset that lacks a clear builder, historical origin, motive, civil function, or political consequence.
> 2. Black Vault and primary-source UAP research, including Black Vault UFO Files Releases #1-#5, the current NARA RG 615 FAA/NRC/ODNI/OSD/NSA/State/FBI series, NARA live-catalog versus bulk-export version skew, NARA legacy Blue Book/Air Intelligence/4602D/OSI/Condon/Roswell/Avrocar collections, the FY2025 AARO report, AARO media-provenance classes, the 2025 narrative-data workshop, KONA BLUE, ORNL material analyses, Evaluation Proposal 585, PWA192, the Immaculate Constellation search appeal, FBI contactee files, the Virginia maritime report, Space Tiger Team, 78 withheld Navy photographs, NASA confirmed-life communications planning, NOAA email records, FOIA appeals, records-retention gaps, Project Moon Dust/fragology, and 2026 AARO imagery: Project Sign, Grudge, Blue Book, Robertson Panel, Tehran 1976, Nimitz, Gimbal, GoFast, Aguadilla, USS Omaha, nuclear and missile-site records, UAPTF/AARO charters, classification guides, FOIA denials, missing attachments, NARA Record Group 615, DIA/AAWSAP DIRDs, CIA, NSA, FBI, Navy, Air Force, and NASA records. Treat The Black Vault as a discovery index and prefer underlying government records.
> 3. Primary contactee and UFO-religion genealogy: George Van Tassel, Robert Short, Ethel P. Hill, Tuella, Ashtar Command, Sananda traditions, Sheldan Nidle, later Galactic Federation of Light material, failed-prophecy adaptation, evacuation theology, and academic UFO-religion scholarship.
> 4. A complete contextual concordance of the Law of One/Ra Material for Confederation structure, Council of Saturn, Orion elite recruitment, quarantine, free-will loopholes, identity appropriation, social-memory complexes, densities, Mars, Maldek, Atlantis, physical craft, thought-forms, Men in Black, technology transfer, and spiritual risks. Treat the corpus as channeled literature, not verified history.
> 5. Mesopotamian primary texts and scholarship on Anunna/Anunnaki, Igigi, Atrahasis, divine labor, Enki, Enlil, Nibiru/neberu, and later Sitchin reinterpretations. Produce a line-by-line mutation map showing what was ancient, what Sitchin added, and what the internet added later.
> 6. Religion as contact, translation, control, and technology: Gnostic Archons, cargo cults, egregores, Monroe's Loosh, Castaneda's Flyers, Bramley's Custodians, Vallée's control-system hypothesis, intelligence disinformation, synthetic revelation, counterfeit identity, and the eight-layer Revelation Stack. Preserve the possibility of authentic spiritual experience.
> 7. Ocean, underground, and orphaned-machine research: Sanderson, the Shaver Mystery, USO and transmedium cases, sonar and heat constraints, seafloor mapping, undersea cables, artifact SETI, Bracewell probes, lurker searches, self-replicating probes, the Silurian Hypothesis, autonomous defense, and long-duration legal archives.
> 8. Current astronomy and plausible forecasts through 2099: Planet Nine, 'Oumuamua, 2I/Borisov, 3I/ATLAS, Apophis and OSIRIS-APEX, Roman, Rubin, Artemis, Dragonfly, Europa Clipper, habitable-world direct imaging, commercial LEO, lunar power, Mars/Belt settlement, climate adaptation, demographic change, AI governance, synthetic biology, and space-resource law. Clearly separate announced mission plans, projections, inference, and fiction.
> 9. Pirate civilization research using real maritime, free-port, privateering, salvage, refugee-smuggling, trafficking, caravan, diaspora, sanctions-evasion, insurance, and distributed-reputation history. Develop Pirates as a civilization of access rather than comic relief.
> 10. Machine politics, personhood, copied minds, mission interpretation, ecological governance, extinct-author law, version control, archival error correction, and the moral status of models trained from living commanders.
>
> Required deliverables:
>
> - source genealogy and evidence ledger;
> - delta report against lore version 0.3.2 and the current game commit;
> - missing-fidelity audit for every game asset;
> - expanded commander relationships and faction-specific campaign arcs;
> - neutral Vigil chassis codex with original custodial functions;
> - tower and unit provenance corrections;
> - deterministic world-dossier generation rules;
> - 2099 chronology with factual anchors and scenario forecasts;
> - Black Vault/NARA/AARO case-to-lore map, archive-version comparison, media-integrity ledger, proposal-program-operation audit, and release-by-release OCR/provenance audit;
> - contradiction register and Two-Key interpretations;
> - at least 100 concise mission seeds tagged to exact game maps, commanders, units, towers, and world kinds;
> - proposed edits only where they preserve stable IDs, mechanics, deterministic generation, save compatibility, and multiplayer protocol;
> - visual briefs for every commander, faction unit, Vigil family, tower family, battlefield, and world-holder variant;
> - at least 25 additional Archive War missions tied to exact live assets and evidence labels;
> - a named-historical-figure review ensuring real people are used only as supported authors, witnesses, archivists, scholars, memorials, or protocol namesakes.
>
> Conduct three passes: broad discovery; provenance and contamination analysis; canon synthesis. End with an adversarial audit identifying every place the report may have treated classification, missing data, popularity, sincere belief, or unresolved status as proof.
