<!-- Source module: docs/research/uap-archive-expansion-2026.md -->

# UAP Archive Expansion - Black Vault, NARA, and AARO (2026)

This addendum records material that became available or materially clearer after the initial 0.3.0 archive pass and the first 0.3.1 expansion. It adds fidelity to the **Archive War**, the Human Open Archive, Federation disclosure policy, Pirate information markets, Compact provenance poisoning, and Machine record law. It does not change the five-faction canon or convert any unresolved report into extraterrestrial evidence.

## What was genuinely new

| Finding | Evidence | What it establishes | What it does not establish | Best use in the setting |
|---|---:|---|---|---|
| **NARA Record Group 615 expanded to seven named agency series** | A | The current RG 615 page lists FAA, NRC, ODNI, OSD, NSA, Department of State, and FBI series, with further transfers expected on a rolling basis. | Completeness, immediate online availability, or truth of every allegation inside a transferred record. | The Open Archive is a growing interagency graph, not one disclosure dump. Every agency brings different custody rules, vocabularies, and omissions. |
| **The April 2025 NARA bulk-download page is no longer the complete agency inventory** | A | The bulk page remains useful for downloadable ZIP and JSON packages, but its visible agency bundles predate the later NSA, State, and FBI series now listed in RG 615. | That the newer series have downloadable bulk packages yet. | Archive interfaces can disagree without either being fraudulent: one is a current holdings index, another is a lagging export surface. |
| **NARA's cross-record-group guide is broader than RG 615** | A | NARA separately indexes UFO/UAP-related records across legacy holdings including FAA, NASA, CIA, OSD, Air Force, Navy, public-diplomacy, broadcasting, and newsreel collections. | That the records describe one program or share one attribution. | The Open Archive gains an **Institutional Shadow Map** showing how one subject leaves different traces in operations, intelligence, science, public affairs, and popular memory. |
| **Known fabricated UFO photographs are preserved as scientific-study records** | A | NARA catalog entries explicitly identify physically fabricated and optically fabricated UFO photographs created or analyzed during scientific study. | That adjacent images are fake, or that archived photographic labels settle every case. | The Human archive retains a **Known-Fake Control Set** for training image analysts, testing provenance tools, and detecting when a derivative has been separated from its original explanatory context. |
| **NARA transfer rules preserve both releasable and restricted copies** | A | Federal guidance requires agencies to identify UAP records and transfer digital copies; publicly releasable and restricted versions can move together while public access remains limited to releasable material. | That a withheld version contains extraordinary evidence. | A document may have several lawful states: public copy, redacted copy, sealed original, metadata-only record, or on-site-only holding. |
| **Black Vault UFO Files search now covers Releases 1-5** | A as publication history / B for underlying records | The derivative search corpus currently indexes 375 records: 212 documents, 133 videos, and 30 images, with processed PDF text, metadata, and some transcripts. | That OCR is accurate, that the corpus includes all older Black Vault holdings, or that the records support one common explanation. | Vanta's archive needs OCR confidence, source-file identity, release batch, and transcript provenance as separate fields. |
| **AARO FY2025 active-archive model** | A/B | AARO received 319 reports, resolved 114 of those plus 256 earlier cases, referred nine for further technical analysis, and placed 191 in an active archive pending better corroboration. | That active-archive cases are exotic. The category explicitly means insufficient information for a comprehensive assessment. | The game can distinguish **resolved**, **technical-review**, **active-archive**, **merged-incident**, and **withheld** states instead of one “unresolved” bucket. |
| **Satellite-flare modeling resolved a large historical batch** | A | AARO reports that a new three-dimensional modeling capability enabled 238 case resolutions as satellite flaring across its holdings. | That every orb or light report is a satellite. | Better tools can change old cases without changing the original witnesses; Archive War missions can reopen records when analytical capability improves. |
| **The Virginia maritime report is a mixed-domain counting problem** | B | AARO describes one maritime-domain report involving about 100 airborne observations and two likely uncrewed surface systems, still under investigation at the report cutoff. | A submerged base, transmedium craft, one coordinated swarm, or extraterrestrial origin. | A scenario about object counting, track association, decoys, surface relays, and incompatible domain authorities - not “100 alien craft.” |
| **Fifty nuclear-site incidents were reported as UAS, not UAP** | A | The FY2025 report separately records 50 UAS incidents near nuclear infrastructure, weapons, and launch sites and explicitly says none were reported as UAP. | That the incidents were harmless or fully attributed. | The setting separates drone incursions, espionage, safety threats, and UAP rather than letting the word “nuclear” force an alien interpretation. |
| **Space Tiger Team documents a space/transmedium coordination effort** | B | A released 2023 Joint Staff processing record describes an AARO-led Space Tiger Team concerned with spaceborne and transmedium cases and detection frameworks. | Results, detected craft, or confirmation that any event crossed domains. | Precursor to Human all-domain fusion cells and the jurisdictional conflicts between space, ocean, air, intelligence, and civil agencies. |
| **The Navy identified 78 responsive UAP photographs and withheld them** | B, administrative | A Navy FOIA appeal record confirms responsive photographs existed and remained classified under the UAP classification framework, with no segregable portion released. | What the photographs depict or whether they are anomalous. | **Negative-space evidence:** existence, classification, content, and interpretation are four different facts. |
| **NASA revisited communications planning for a confirmed extraterrestrial-life discovery** | B for the released planning records | A NASA FOIA release documents a June 2025 discussion about how to communicate a definitive discovery of extraterrestrial life. | That a discovery occurred or that a final protocol was approved. | The institutional ancestor of the 2099 **First Contact Communications Protocol**, including who speaks first, what counts as confirmation, and how uncertainty is presented. |
| **NOAA email records support interest, not a hidden ocean-UAP program** | B | A released NOAA email set documents UAP-related keyword hits and professional interest associated with Timothy Gallaudet. | Direct membership in the UAP Task Force, a NOAA-led USO program, or confirmation of later public claims. | Keeps ocean lore disciplined: an official's interest and network are not the same as an agency mission. |
| **The “Immaculate Constellation” appeal demonstrates allegation records** | B/E | An initial refusal to search was appealed successfully, so a records search would occur even though the agency denied the alleged program existed. | Existence of the alleged program. | Records about a false, disputed, or invented claim may exist because officials, Congress, media, and investigators discussed it. Search results cannot be equated with program confirmation. |
| **Deleted or missing email accounts can be partially reconstructed through correspondents** | B, administrative | Black Vault's reconstructed email archive demonstrates that lost custody on one side of a communication can sometimes be mitigated by requesting the other participants' records. | Completeness or authenticity of claims absent contemporaneous records. | The **Mirror Archive Method**: every important communication is sought from sender, recipient, attachment owner, calendar, routing log, and records office. |
| **Project Moon Dust and “fragology” provide a real recovery-program template** | A/B | Historical records describe programs concerned with recovering and analyzing foreign or unidentified reentry debris. | Alien crash retrieval. | The mundane recovery apparatus is the procedural ancestor of later Human foreign-materiel and anomalous-material teams; rumor mutates real recovery logistics into a universal alien-crash story. |
| **FAA PWA192 records preserve both corroboration and a major data gap** | B | Released FAA logs and ATC audio document a May 20, 2025 report of an unidentified aircraft-like object pacing a business jet over Nevada; radar files were withheld. | Exotic origin, exact kinematics, or absence from every sensor. | A mission where narrative, audio, operations log, radar custody, and nearby test ranges remain separate evidence layers. |
| **Direct measurement and derived interpretation are different evidence objects** | A | Current NPS guidance emphasizes that range, speed, acceleration, size, temperature, reflectivity, and similar conclusions depend on metadata, calibration, geometry, and assumptions. It recommends multiple separated sensors when possible. | That a calculated value is trustworthy merely because software produced it. | Every combat-intelligence card can expose the assumption chain behind a threat estimate, allowing opponents to attack metadata, calibration, or correlation rather than the witness. |
| **Native files, hashes, and derivatives need separate custody** | A | Current practical guidance recommends immediate hashing, immutable or WORM-style storage, original unprocessed files, UTC/location/equipment logs, calibration frames, and clearly labeled derivatives. | That an intact file proves an extraordinary interpretation. | The Human Open Archive and Machine courts treat a native master, an authenticated derivative, and a viral edit as related but legally distinct objects. |
| **Event-based sensing turns identification into a staged process** | A/speculative application | Sandia's WATCHER pipeline has been demonstrated on known drone data using event-based sensors to detect, track, estimate motion, and characterize periodic rotor signatures. | A detected extraterrestrial craft, a universal classifier, or an operational planetary surveillance network. | The 2099 **Watcher Mesh** begins every contact as an unidentified event cluster and gains identity only as custody, geometry, and signature detail accumulate. |
| **Classification may protect the sensor rather than the object** | A | AARO explains that even imagery of ordinary birds, balloons, drones, or natural phenomena may remain classified because raw data reveal platform, location, metadata, resolution, or sensitive collection capability. | That withholding implies an extraordinary subject. | Archive War missions track a protected source and a mundane or unresolved subject separately, preventing classification itself from becoming an alien-evidence stat. |
| **A reporting form must permit uncertainty instead of forcing completion** | A | The 2025 narrative-data workshop recommended provenance, flexible structured and narrative fields, optional metadata, human review, and “refuse to answer” choices. | That form design makes testimony objective or eliminates deception. | Human and Federation investigators treat an explicit refusal, missing memory, unknown value, inaccessible record, and negative observation as five different states. |

## Second-pass additions accepted in 0.3.2

| Finding | Evidence | What it establishes | What it does not establish | Lore adaptation |
|---|---:|---|---|---|
| **Media provenance is part of the evidence** | A/B | AARO labels some releases as secondary recordings of displays, some as digitally altered before intake, and some as artistic interpretations of testimony. | That low-fidelity material is false, or that all official media are equivalent to native sensor data. | **Media Integrity Ladder** and parent-child evidence graphs. |
| **Evaluation Proposal 585** | B | A released inspector-general proposal states the UAP intelligence and counterintelligence workflow it intended to evaluate; benefit and capability sections remain classified. | The content of the redactions or an extraordinary finding. | **Blind Metrics:** players may know the audit question without knowing the result or protected capability. |
| **KONA BLUE maturity boundary** | A/E | AARO says the prospective compartment was proposed but never approved, established, funded, or supplied with material. | An operational retrieval program. | **Proposal-Program-Operation Ladder** prevents code names and briefing decks from becoming proof of implementation. |
| **ORNL ordinary-alloy result** | A/B | A claimed specimen can be tested and found consistent with ordinary industrial alloy. | That every provenance story is fabricated or that every alleged specimen is ordinary. | Negative results and chain of custody become valuable mission outcomes rather than failed content. |
| **Narrative-data infrastructure** | A | AARO sponsored an August 2025 workshop on narrative reports, infrastructure, and analysis. | That testimony equals calibrated sensor data or is analytically useless. | Human archives preserve original wording, interview context, contradiction, and later mutation as separate objects. |
| **FBI contactee and paranormal files** | B/C/D | Released files document correspondence, monitoring, fraud concerns, organizations, investigators, and public controversy around UFO and contactee movements. | FBI endorsement of contact claims. | A historical network map for Space Brother, Ashtar-adjacent, and counter-contact traditions. |
| **State Department RG 615 lane** | A/B | Diplomatic records now form a distinct transferred series in the live RG 615 inventory. | Official endorsement of reports transmitted through diplomatic channels. | **Diplomatic Sky:** translation, local political context, embassy routing, and treaty consequences enter the Archive War. |
| **NARA legacy corpus remains strategically useful** | A/B | The bulk surface includes operational, investigative, scientific, photographic, audiovisual, and presidential collections far beyond the four current electronic agency bundles. | One unified disclosure project. | Archive missions can trace how vocabulary, jurisdiction, and analysis changed across institutions and decades. |

## FY2025 AARO details that matter for game logic

The report supports several useful distinctions:

1. **Report count is not incident count.** AARO merges reports when multiple submissions correspond to one event.
2. **Location density reflects sensor and reporting density.** More reports near U.S. military operations do not automatically mean more anomalous activity there.
3. **Narrative claims and technical validation are different evidence objects.** Some reports near critical infrastructure described apparently advanced behavior but lacked accompanying technical data.
4. **Materials procedures can exist before materials do.** AARO says no evidence indicates a U.S. public or private entity captured or exploited UAP-derived material, while also developing a process should material ever be received.
5. **Analysis changes over time.** New modeling methods can resolve old cases, which means an archive is an active analytical system rather than a tomb.
6. **The active archive is not a “mystery confirmed” list.** It is a queue of insufficiently supported cases retained for pattern analysis and possible reopening.

These distinctions should become explicit fields in Archive War content:

```text
record_status:
  raw_report | correlated_incident | resolved | technical_review |
  active_archive | withheld | no_records | custody_gap | appealed

attribution_status:
  identified | probable | unresolved_identity | unresolved_behavior |
  insufficient_data | disputed | not_assessed

provenance_quality:
  original | agency_copy | derivative_release | OCR_text |
  transcript | commentary | reconstruction

media_integrity:
  native_sensor | documented_export | secondary_display_capture |
  altered_copy | artistic_reconstruction | narrative_only

program_maturity:
  allegation | suggestion | briefing | proposed_compartment |
  approved_program | funded_operation | material_received | result_produced
```

## New lore concepts accepted

### Sensor Custody Doctrine

The 2099 Open Archive records observations before conclusions. Every derived velocity, size, acceleration, or trajectory remains linked to the sensor geometry, platform motion, calibration, metadata, assumptions, processing steps, and confidence interval that produced it. An adversary can therefore compromise an analysis without forging the original image.

### Native Master and Fork Law

The first immutable hash defines the **native master**. Every crop, stabilized clip, denoised image, contrast adjustment, transcript, OCR layer, reconstruction, and briefing slide is a fork with its own author and transformation history. Federation memory courts and Machine Mission Courts disagree over whether an authenticated fork can ever acquire equal legal status to its source.

### The Watcher Mesh

Human and Machine persistent-monitoring systems inherit event-based sensing rather than a magical all-seeing radar. They detect motion and periodic signatures efficiently, but identity still depends on baseline geometry, focus, calibration, correlation, and continuing custody. The network's most dangerous failure is not missing an object; it is assigning a political identity before the track has earned one.

### Refusal Is Data

By 2099, investigators distinguish `no`, `unknown`, `not remembered`, `not observed`, `withheld`, `not applicable`, and `refuse to answer`. Compact systems deliberately collapse those states into whatever answer makes a dependency contract appear complete.

### Institutional Shadow Map

The same subject can generate records in aviation safety, intelligence, military operations, scientific research, public diplomacy, news production, and records administration. These are not duplicates to collapse. They are institutional views with different purposes, vocabularies, custody rules, and blind spots.

### Known-Fake Control Set

Fabricated, staged, optically altered, and misidentified examples are preserved rather than discarded. Their value is calibration: a classifier, analyst, or noetic authentication system that cannot recognize known deceptions is not ready to judge unknown material. A control image never proves that an unrelated case is false; it tests whether the method can detect the kinds of error it claims to detect.

### The Open Archive is a versioned graph

Humanity's Open Archive does not store “the truth” as one file. It stores relationships among:

- original reports;
- incident mergers;
- sensor files;
- witness statements;
- agency copies;
- classification decisions;
- redactions;
- appeals;
- derivative OCR;
- transcripts;
- later analytical resolutions;
- missing or destroyed custodial records.

A record can be authentic while a claim inside it is mistaken. A transcript can be accurate while the speaker is wrong. A classified photograph can exist while its content remains unknowable. A “no records” response can describe a poor search, lawful destruction, wrong custodian, or true absence.

### The Federation's First Contact Communications Protocol

The NASA planning material becomes a factual institutional ancestor, not proof of contact. In fiction, the Federation judges a civilization partly by whether it can announce a discovery without:

- allowing one government to monopolize it;
- declaring speculation as confirmation;
- erasing minority religious interpretations;
- triggering avoidable panic;
- hiding uncertainty behind authority;
- letting a counterfeit transmission become the first global narrative.

Ashtar's first public transmission is therefore preceded by a conflict over **who is permitted to authenticate the speaker**, not simply whether the message can be heard.

### Negative-space records

Some assets are known only through denial letters, classification guides, transfer logs, indexes, attachment lists, or references in another office's file. They are not blank checks for extraordinary lore. They create constrained mission objects:

- prove that a record existed;
- locate the lawful custodian;
- determine why it was withheld;
- preserve its hash and chain of custody;
- distinguish source protection from content sensitivity;
- avoid inventing the unseen contents.

### The Mirror Archive Method

When a primary mailbox or file series is missing, investigators seek copies from every participant and system that could have retained it. This directly supports gameplay in which one faction destroys an archive but cannot erase:

- recipient copies;
- transit logs;
- calendar invitations;
- attachment hashes;
- backup indexes;
- legal-review records;
- downstream quotation;
- Machine memory witnesses.

### Recovery teams do not imply alien wreckage

Project Moon Dust and related debris-analysis history provide the correct procedural base for Human recovery units: field security, foreign-materiel exploitation, hazardous-material control, technical identification, diplomacy, and custody. The fictional leap is that a later team eventually receives genuinely nonhuman material. The historical program is not retroactively made extraterrestrial.

### Media Integrity Ladder

A media object is not defined only by what it depicts. The archive records which instrument generated it, whether the public file is a native export, whether another camera recorded a display, what alterations predated intake, and whether the image is only a reconstruction. Lower-fidelity material can preserve valuable testimony while supporting fewer kinematic or morphological claims.

### Proposal-Program-Operation Ladder

A code name can exist at several maturity levels: allegation, suggestion, briefing, proposed compartment, approved program, funded operation, material receipt, and produced result. KONA BLUE is the control case: the proposal existed, while AARO says the program was never established or supplied with material.

### Blind Metrics

An evaluation can disclose what an institution intends to measure while protecting why the measure matters, which capabilities are vulnerable, or what results were found. Classification of method cannot be used as a substitute for evidence about the evaluated phenomenon.

### Narrative Infrastructure

The Open Archive stores exact witness language, interviewer prompts, agency summaries, illustrations, media exposure, confidence, contradiction, and later retelling as linked layers. This preserves testimony without silently converting memory into sensor data.

### Null Search Paradox

Program existence, phrase occurrence, government discussion, congressional inquiry, and responsive records are separate propositions. Winning an appeal that forces a search establishes a lawful search obligation, not the truth of the underlying allegation.

## High-value Archive War hooks

| Hook | Faction pressure | Battlefield or system fit |
|---|---|---|
| **The Seven Custodians** - the same incident appears in seven RG 615 agency series with incompatible redaction logic. | Humans want one open graph; Federation wants protected witness consent; Pirates sell missing attachments; Xenos poison cross-agency identity; Machines preserve every version. | `lattice`, `crossroads`, or a seven-world system dossier. |
| **Active Archive 191** - a new model can resolve most of a backlog, but training it on classified sensor behavior exposes defense capabilities. | Humans split over transparency; Federation offers privacy-preserving analysis; Pirates possess civilian comparison data; Xenos seed adversarial examples; Machines demand immutable inputs. | `orrery` or `lattice`. |
| **Seventy-Eight Frames** - the players know images exist but cannot see them. | Victory comes from custody and classification analysis, not guessing the pictures. | `ossuary` or `crown`. |
| **The Surface Pair** - two uncrewed surface systems may be relays, unrelated vessels, or the only trackable part of a larger event. | Old Weather is one hypothesis among several; no faction receives automatic attribution. | `atoll`, `delta`, or `strait`. |
| **First Words** - a confirmed biosignature, technosignature, or contact signal must be announced before a hostile actor supplies a counterfeit narrative. | Federation consent doctrine collides with Human sovereignty and Pirate leak culture. | `confluence` or `maelstrom`. |
| **Search the Allegation** - officials deny a named program but still possess records discussing the allegation. | Xenos exploit the difference between “records exist” and “program exists.” | `crown` or `crossroads`. |
| **The Other Mailbox** - a destroyed archive is reconstructed from correspondents and routing systems. | Machines excel at persistence; Pirates control copies; Humans need admissible provenance. | `loom` or `lattice`. |
| **Moon Dust Protocol** - a recovery team races to identify fallen material before five factions rewrite its origin. | Ordinary debris can still be strategically sensitive; exotic attribution is earned only by analysis. | `shattered`, `expanse`, or `anvil`. |
| **Pacing Track** - witness audio says one thing, an operations log says another, and radar remains withheld. | The player must preserve uncertainty while preventing a safety incident. | `lance` or `skew`. |
| **The Control Set** - known fabricated and optically altered photographs are detached from their explanatory metadata and recirculated as fresh evidence. | Humans need a defensible training corpus; Federation auditors demand consent and context; Pirates trade the unlabeled copies; Xenos weaponize classifier overconfidence; Machines preserve every fork. | `shattered`, `loom`, or `causeway`. |

| **Native or Recreated** - a vivid “official video” is an artistic visualization attached to a real report. | Every faction can exploit the public assumption that publication equals native evidence. | `shattered` or `rift`. |
| **Proposal Not Program** - a dramatic code name survives after the proposal that created it failed. | Humans need administrative history; Xenos need ambiguity; Pirates sell the deck; Machines demand proof of execution. | `ossuary` or `anvil`. |
| **Diplomatic Sky** - the same event crosses embassy cables, local media, defense reporting, and religious interpretation. | Translation and political context matter as much as sensor quality. | `crossroads` or `confluence`. |
| **Contactee Network** - files document a movement's relationships without authenticating its claimed visitors. | The mission separates belief history, fraud inquiry, surveillance, sincere experience, and later myth. | `rift` or `lattice`. |

## Archive-mining workflow

Do **not** download hundreds of gigabytes before reading catalog metadata.

1. Use the current RG 615 page to identify the agency series and NARA IDs.
2. Use the bulk page for packages that actually exist as ZIP/JSON exports.
3. Ingest JSON metadata first and deduplicate by NARA ID, file hash, agency case number, incident time, and source URL.
4. Keep original government files separate from Black Vault processed/OCR derivatives.
5. Record OCR quality and never quote a garbled scan without checking the image.
6. Distinguish event date, report date, transfer date, release date, publication date, and appeal date.
7. Preserve denial letters, appeal outcomes, “no records” determinations, and missing-attachment notes as first-class records.
8. Link, but do not merge, records that may describe the same event until time, location, platform, and provenance support the correlation.
9. Extract game-design tags only after the factual case summary and limitations are written.
10. Recheck current AARO and RG 615 pages before each lore release because both are rolling collections.

## Canon boundary

Nothing in this addendum establishes extraterrestrial origin, ocean bases, reverse-engineered craft, hidden treaties, or a unified secret program. The lore uses archival structure, procedural ambiguity, sensor limitations, and public-trust problems as historical scaffolding. The nonhuman history remains **F** unless a source is explicitly identified as another evidence class.

# UAP Archive Delta - August 2026

This is the concise second-pass delta carried into lore release 0.3.2. It adds archival methods and scenario material, not a new claim that UAP records establish extraterrestrial activity.

## What changed

| New source lane | What is established | What is not established | Lore value |
|---|---|---|---|
| **NARA RG 615 live collection vs bulk-download page** | The live Record Group 615 page now lists FAA, NRC, ODNI, OSD, NSA, State, and FBI series on a rolling basis. The separate bulk-download page still packages only the first four electronic series and remains dated April 24, 2025. | That a missing bulk ZIP means a record was withheld for extraordinary reasons, or that every item in a transferred series is accurate. | **Archive Version Skew:** the catalog, bulk snapshot, agency transfer, processed mirror, and local index may all be valid while describing different release states. |
| **Black Vault UFO Files Releases 1-5** | The searchable derivative now covers 375 records: 212 documents, 133 videos, and 30 images across five release batches. It includes processed OCR, transcripts where available, metadata, and offline ZIPs. | That OCR text is error-free, that a derivative replaces the government original, or that this five-release corpus contains the older Black Vault UAP archive. | A model for Vanta's Open Archive: searchable derivatives remain linked to originals, hashes, release batches, and processing history. |
| **AARO 2026 imagery and report releases** | The public collection now mixes native or near-native sensor products, secondary recordings of displays, digitally altered copies presented as received, cropped witness footage, narrative descriptions, and explicit artistic recreations. | That every published video has equal evidentiary value, or that a recreation is footage of the reported event. | **Media Integrity Ladder:** game dossiers record generation, custody, transformation, and analytical status separately. |
| **PWA192, May 20, 2025** | FAA records and ATC audio document a business-jet report over northern Nevada; witness language evolved during the encounter, no radar target was reported in the released log, and twelve ERAM radar files were withheld as Sensitive Security Information. | The object's identity. Nearby military ranges make a classified or military asset plausible, but not proven. | A mission about description drift, missing sensor access, air-safety response, and competing black-program, foreign-platform, conventional, and exotic hypotheses. |
| **Evaluation Proposal 585** | A released DoW OIG proposal states an objective to assess DoD intelligence and counterintelligence actions for detecting, collecting, exploiting, analyzing, and addressing UAP. Core benefit and capability language remains redacted. | That the redactions conceal extraterrestrial conclusions. | **Blind Metrics:** the player must distinguish a classified evaluation method from the thing being evaluated. |
| **Immaculate Constellation FOIA dispute** | A January 2026 response declined to search for a phrase because the alleged subject was said not to exist; a May 26 appeal was granted and a search was ordered. | The existence of the alleged program. Records can discuss an allegation even when the allegation is false. | **Null Search Paradox:** program existence, phrase occurrence, official discussion, and responsive records are four different questions. |
| **KONA BLUE and ORNL material analyses** | AARO states KONA BLUE was a proposed prospective special-access program that was never approved, established, funded, or supplied with material. ORNL found a claimed Ohio specimen consistent with ordinary aluminum alloy; another magnesium-alloy analysis likewise documents testable material claims. | That a proposal was an operating program, or that an unusual story makes an ordinary specimen exotic. | **Proposal-Program-Operation Ladder** and **Chain of Custody Missions** prevent slides, code names, or provenance stories from outranking implementation and laboratory results. |
| **FBI and contactee-movement files** | The Black Vault's FBI paranormal collection preserves records concerning contactees, UFO organizations, skeptics, investigators, broadcasters, and disputed documents. | FBI endorsement of the underlying contact claims. An FBI file may reflect correspondence, fraud concerns, security review, public controversy, or routine recordkeeping. | A source lane for the historical development of Space Brother, Ashtar-adjacent, contactee, and counter-contact narratives without turning surveillance into validation. |
| **AARO narrative-data workshop and 2026 CTX issue** | Current official material treats narrative reports, reporting standards, infrastructure, data quality, and analysis as research problems in their own right. | That witness narratives are useless or that standardized reporting resolves ontology automatically. | The institutional ancestor of the Human **Narrative Infrastructure Office**, which preserves testimony while separating it from sensor and attribution claims. |

## Archive rules added to canon support

### Archive Version Skew

Never treat one page, mirror, ZIP, catalog entry, or local index as the archive's final state. Record:

1. agency transfer date;
2. NARA catalog state;
3. bulk-snapshot date;
4. secondary processing date;
5. local ingestion date;
6. later correction, appeal, or replacement.

### Media Integrity Ladder

Every image or video receives one of these provenance classes:

1. **Native sensor product** - original or validated export with sensor context.
2. **Documented export or transcode** - transformed, with a known conversion chain.
3. **Secondary display capture** - a camera recorded another display; added artifacts are expected.
4. **Altered copy, presented as received** - annotations, replay, enhancement, speed changes, or edits predate archival intake.
5. **Artistic reconstruction** - a visualization of testimony, not event footage.
6. **Narrative-only record** - no surviving visual object is available.

A lower-integrity class is not automatically false. It answers fewer questions.

### Proposal-Program-Operation Ladder

A name may refer to:

1. a suggestion;
2. a briefing deck;
3. a proposed compartment or PSAP;
4. an approved program;
5. a funded and staffed operation;
6. an operation that actually received material or produced results.

Game writing must never skip levels merely because a title sounds classified.

### Narrative Infrastructure

Witness testimony remains valuable, but it is stored beside rather than inside sensor conclusions. Reports preserve:

- exact original wording;
- later retellings;
- illustrations and media that influenced memory;
- interviewer prompts;
- agency summaries;
- confidence and uncertainty;
- contradictions that have not been reconciled.

## Faction applications

| Faction | New archive use |
|---|---|
| **Humans** | Build an Open Archive that preserves raw, processed, and disputed versions rather than promising one disclosure dump will settle history. |
| **Federation** | Authenticate contact through complete memory-witness chains and disclose whether a message is direct, translated, reconstructed, or symbolic. |
| **Xenos** | Exploit version skew, derivative media, and proposal-language to manufacture proof-shaped dependency without forging every underlying record. |
| **Pirates** | Trade missing attachments, original hashes, deprecated indexes, and appeal results as strategic route intelligence. |
| **Machines** | Treat every transformation as a forked state with its own custody history, while risking paralysis when no version can be declared final. |

## Second-pass additions: data quality, sensor custody, and classification

| New source lane | What is established | What is not established | Lore value |
|---|---|---|---|
| **NPS data-vetting guidance** | Direct measurements and derived quantities must be separated. Estimates of range, speed, size, temperature, reflectivity, or radar cross-section require sensor-specific metadata; two separated optical sensors can provide triangulation and help reject near-field confusers. | That a multi-sensor report is automatically extraordinary, or that processed imagery preserves native photometric properties. | **Measurement Lineage:** every dossier records the raw observation, the assumptions used, the derived value, and whether another analyst can reproduce it. |
| **Native-master evidence protocol** | Current technical guidance recommends preserving original unprocessed files, hashing immediately after capture, keeping high-bitrate or RAW masters, logging UTC/location/equipment metadata, and labeling derivatives. | That a cryptographically intact file proves the depicted object is exotic. Hashes prove file integrity, not interpretation. | **Native Master Rule:** a faction may argue over meaning, but it cannot silently overwrite the evidence object from which the argument was derived. |
| **Event-based sensing and WATCHER** | Sandia's WATCHER pipeline demonstrates detection, tracking, and characterization of drone signatures in event-based sensor data. Event-based sensing can offer very fast response, low bandwidth, high dynamic range, and persistent monitoring. The cited demonstrations used known UAS targets. | Detection of alien technology or a finished universal UAP classifier. An initially unidentified cluster is a workflow state that may later become a known target. | **Provisional Unknown:** game sensors create tracks before identities. Players gain advantage by maintaining custody long enough to characterize periodic, linear, reflective, biological, or platform-motion signatures. |
| **AARO declassification guidance** | Imagery can remain classified because of the collecting platform, location, metadata, resolution, or source-and-method implications even when the depicted object is ordinary. The originating agency, not AARO alone, controls declassification. | That classified imagery depicts an extraordinary object. | **Source-Subject Split:** classification is tracked separately for the observed subject, collection platform, location, analytical method, and operational context. |
| **Narrative reporting design** | AARO workshop participants recommended structured and narrative fields, provenance, optional metadata, human review, and an explicit “refuse to answer” option rather than pressuring witnesses to fabricate missing details. | That a standardized form removes memory limits, hoaxes, or interpretive disagreement. | **Refusal Is Data:** an unanswered field is distinguishable from a negative answer, forgotten detail, inaccessible record, or analyst assumption. |

### Measurement Lineage

Every computed claim must retain:

1. the direct measurement;
2. sensor and platform metadata;
3. calibration state;
4. assumptions;
5. transformation or processing history;
6. derived quantity;
7. uncertainty range;
8. independent reproduction status.

### Native Master Rule

Original files are hashed at intake and stored as immutable masters. Crops, contrast adjustments, stabilization, frame interpolation, denoising, transcription, OCR, and visual reconstruction are separate descendants. No derivative inherits the master's evidentiary class automatically.

### Provisional Unknown

“Unidentified” is a temporary analytical state, not a biological category. A track may move through these statuses:

`detected -> custody maintained -> characterized -> correlated -> identified`

or:

`detected -> custody lost -> active archive`

This becomes a core Archive War mechanic: preserving observation continuity can be more valuable than firing first.

### Source-Subject Split

Every withholding or classification record should specify which element is protected when known:

- observed subject;
- collection platform;
- sensor capability;
- location or operational pattern;
- analytic method;
- foreign-partner information;
- witness identity;
- unresolved combination of the above.

## What was deliberately not added

- No new historical case was declared extraterrestrial.
- No classified or withheld record was treated as proof of extraordinary content.
- No artistic recreation was treated as event footage.
- No proposed program was promoted into an operating program.
- No FBI or diplomatic file was treated as endorsement of the allegation it records.
- No NARA catalog lag was converted into a cover-up claim.

## Primary and discovery routes

- NARA Record Group 615: <https://www.archives.gov/research/topics/uaps/rg-615>
- NARA UAP bulk downloads: <https://www.archives.gov/research/catalog/catalog-bulk-downloads/uap-bulk-download>
- AARO UAP report documents and imagery: <https://www.aaro.mil/Next-AARO-Home-redesign/Next-Parent/Next-UAP-Report-Documents/>
- AARO UAP records and information papers: <https://www.aaro.mil/UAP-Records/>
- Black Vault UFO Files search: <https://www.theblackvault.com/documentarchive/ufo-files-search-engine/>
- Black Vault document archive: <https://www.theblackvault.com/documentarchive/>
- PWA192 FAA record article: <https://www.theblackvault.com/documentarchive/faa-records-detail-pilot-encounter-with-unidentified-object-pacing-aircraft-over-nevada/>
- Evaluation Proposal 585 article and records: <https://www.theblackvault.com/documentarchive/department-of-war-cites-national-security-to-hide-core-uap-evaluation-details/>
- Immaculate Constellation FOIA search dispute: <https://www.theblackvault.com/documentarchive/pentagon-refuses-to-search-for-immaculate-constellation-emails/>
- FBI paranormal collection: <https://www.theblackvault.com/documentarchive/fbi-files-the-paranormal-collection/>

# Research Materials Map

This page inventories the preserved research behind the maintained lore. Presence in the repository does **not** make a source or draft binding canon.

2. Use the [Source Ledger](source-ledger.md) for maintained provenance summaries.
3. Read the [2026 UAP Archive Expansion](uap-archive-expansion-2026.md) for the latest NARA, AARO, and Black Vault delta.
4. Read the [raw deep-research report](../../research/raw/deep-research-report-2026-08-25.md) when you need the complete dossier and scenario inventory.

## Promotion rule

Archived wording can be quoted, challenged, split, or discarded. It becomes canon only when a pull request:

- identifies the evidence class;
- states what is source-derived and what is fictional synthesis;
- updates the maintained lore page;
- updates any affected JSON mirror;
- passes `python scripts/validate_lore.py` and `mkdocs build --strict`.

# Alien and Space Mythos Expansion

# Alien and Space Mythos Delta - August 2026

## What this release adds

Lore release **0.3.3** adds a provenance-controlled mythology layer without changing the five factions or the live game simulation.

- **37 myth systems** spanning reptilian-overlord lore, secret societies, depopulation narratives, time travel, psychic warfare, UFO religions, prison-planet systems, persistent anomaly zones, secret-space claims, lunar myths, collider portals, private elite forums, demonological UFO models, and warning entities.
- **15 canon institutions and technologies**, including the Serpent Court, Six Hidden Tables, Nine Keys, Chronology Court, Causal Return Grid, Continuity Calculus, Population Ledger, Coherence Reservoir, Ancestor Vault, Contrail Ledger, Tissue Ledger, Public-Key Landscape, Luminous Ecology, Route Shadow, and Cross-Layer Fault.
- **74 Mythos Operations** suitable for campaign arcs, planet dossiers, commander relationships, Archive War encounters, and dialogue.
- A **40-source mythos register** integrated into the normalized CSV/XLSX evidence matrix.
- An expanded Deep Research prompt for a second provenance pass.
- An **18-episode Inside Job reference map** translating the fictional corpus into non-infringing institutional lessons and 2099 hooks.

## Load-bearing conclusions

### Mega-conspiracy is a merger

The contemporary package of reptilian rulers, occult elites, underground bases, alien treaties, population reduction, mind control, weather weapons, time travel, soul traps, and secret fleets developed through mergers among older fiction, religion, contactee movements, real classified programs, documented abuse, and Internet synthesis. The Archive War should preserve those separate lineages.

### Documented abuse does not validate unlimited extension

Forced sterilization, MKULTRA, covert weather modification, official secrecy, and intelligence disinformation are real historical credibility kernels. They do not establish a unified alien-directed depopulation or mind-control plan.

### Reptilian is morphology; Serpent Court is institution

Reptiloid beings can belong to every faction. The Serpent Court is a Compact continuity institution using adaptive bodies, inherited credentials, and legal succession. No real population is recast as an alien bloodline.

### Time manipulation has four layers

- Retrocognition observes or reconstructs the past.
- Information backflow sends data rather than bodies.
- Branch selection chooses among nearby histories and creates archive forks.
- Physical chronology transit remains rare, expensive, and identity-destabilizing.

### Inside Job is a design corpus

Its useful lesson is not that conspiracies are factual. It is that secret systems require staff, budgets, procurement, maintenance, succession, and personal incentives. No cabal is omniscient; impossible technology still belongs to somebody.

## Where to continue
