# Alien and Space Mythos Atlas - 2026 Research Expansion

## Scope

This atlas maps additional alien, occult, conspiracy, time-travel, hidden-government,
religious, and space-myth traditions that can enrich **Cosmic Conquest** without
turning every rumor into secret history.

The governing research rule is:

> A tradition can preserve a useful image, institutional pattern, fear, or displaced
> operational detail without its extraordinary explanation being historically true.

Evidence labels remain:

- **A - Established core:** verified science, documented history, or an authentic public record.
- **B - Authentic record, unresolved attribution:** the record is real; the extraordinary interpretation is not established.
- **C - Historical tradition:** documented folklore, religion, literature, or belief history.
- **D - Traceable fringe claim:** a named contactee, channeling, alternative-history, or conspiracy source.
- **E - Contaminated claim:** anonymous, forged, circular, admitted-hoax, or severely provenance-damaged material.
- **F - Original 2099 synthesis:** material created for the game.

This document does not endorse claims about reptilian rulers, a unified depopulation
program, time-traveling government children, alien religious founders, or a global
prison-planet system. It traces how those ideas developed and converts their strongest
narrative structures into explicit fiction.

## Executive findings

### 1. The modern mega-conspiracy is a merger, not an ancient revelation

The familiar Internet package - reptilian rulers, secret councils, underground bases,
ancient astronauts, population reduction, mind control, weather weapons, alien-human
hybrids, soul recycling, and time travel - did not originate in one corpus. It formed
through repeated mergers:

1. Nineteenth-century occult lost-race and hidden-world literature.
2. Early twentieth-century weird fiction about serpent infiltrators and subterranean peoples.
3. Postwar saucer contactees, Space Brothers, Men in Black, and secret-government fears.
4. Ancient-astronaut reinterpretations of religion and archaeology.
5. Abduction, reproductive, and hybrid-program literature.
6. Real disclosures about intelligence abuse, psychological experimentation, weather modification,
   covert proposals, and classified aerospace programs.
7. New World Order and apocalyptic political conspiracism.
8. Internet-era soul-trap, simulation, Mandela-effect, and future-human theories.

The setting should preserve those separate genealogies. Their convergence is a feature
of the Archive War, not proof that every tradition described the same actors.

### 2. Real misconduct is the credibility kernel

Several historical facts explain why totalizing stories remain persuasive:

- coercive eugenics and forced sterilization were real;
- intelligence agencies conducted unethical behavioral experiments;
- governments considered or conducted covert deception and weather-modification programs;
- military and intelligence institutions withheld information for genuine security reasons;
- some official files are incomplete, delayed, contradictory, or poorly cataloged.

Those facts do not validate unrelated alien or depopulation claims. They create the
psychological and institutional environment in which a larger hidden-history story can grow.

### 3. Inside Job provides a design method, not evidence

The animated series **Inside Job** is useful because it treats conspiracies as the
maintenance burden of competing, dysfunctional institutions. Its creator has explicitly
said she does not believe a real shadow government exists. The show's most useful design
lesson is that even an organization with impossible technology is staffed by people with
career incentives, grudges, budgets, families, blind spots, and succession problems.

For Cosmic Conquest, the adaptation is:

- no single omniscient cabal;
- several institutions can possess partial truth;
- cover-ups require logistics and fail through ordinary bureaucracy;
- secret rulers can be powerful without being competent;
- continuity errors may be archive forks, spoofing, or timeline intervention;
- character motive remains more important than conspiracy spectacle.

### 4. Population-control lore requires a three-layer separation

The setting must distinguish:

1. **Documented coercion:** eugenics, forced sterilization, ethnic cleansing, reproductive control,
   and other real abuses.
2. **Public policy and elite rhetoric:** sustainability plans, demographic forecasts, pandemic
   exercises, climate programs, and economic initiatives whose actual texts can be examined.
3. **Totalizing conspiracy synthesis:** the unsupported claim that those separate activities are
   one coordinated plan to exterminate most of humanity.

The first category belongs to history. The second belongs to policy analysis. The third
belongs to conspiracy genealogy and must not be narrated as fact.

### 5. Time travel works best as four different technologies

The source traditions become coherent when separated into:

- **Retrocognition:** reconstruction or observation of the past without changing it.
- **Information backflow:** a message, key, memory, or probability correction moves backward.
- **Branch selection:** a system chooses among nearby histories and creates archive discrepancies.
- **Physical chronology transit:** bodies or craft cross time, with severe causal and identity costs.

Most 2099 time manipulation should use the first three. Physical transit remains rare enough
that it cannot solve ordinary military or political problems.

## Historical merger map

| Era | Source families | What enters the later mega-myth |
|---|---|---|
| 1870s-1910s | Vril fiction, Theosophy, Lemuria, occult Agartha, spiritual evolution | Hidden advanced races, buried power, root races, secret masters |
| 1920s-1940s | Serpent-men fiction, Lovecraftian cosmicism, Shaver Mystery, wartime secret technology | Shape-shifting infiltrators, underground machines, ancient surviving intelligences |
| 1947-1960s | Flying-saucer era, contactees, Ashtar, Aetherius, Council of Nine, Men in Black | Space federations, cosmic teachers, secret suppressors, spiritual energy technologies |
| 1960s-1970s | Ancient astronauts, Bermuda Triangle, cattle mutilations, crop-circle precursors, weather modification | Gods-as-aliens, portals, biological sampling, coded landscape messages, environmental weapons |
| 1980s | Abduction literature, Communion, loosh, New Age synthesis, Georgia Guidestones | Grey technicians, reproductive programs, emotional harvesting, depopulation symbolism |
| 1990s | Montauk, UFO-New World Order merger, reptilian elites, prison-planet precursors, chemtrails | Time travel, mind control, underground war, hybrid bloodlines, population control |
| 2000s-2010s | Simulation argument, cryptoterrestrials, soul-net lore, dark-forest popularization, social media | Reality as code, hidden Earth natives, reincarnation trap, hostile silence, recursive rumor |
| 2020s | Official UAP archives, algorithmic media, synthetic content, Inside Job-style synthesis | Archive warfare, spoofed evidence, competing secret institutions, editable reality records |

## Myth-system dossiers

### M-01 - Serpent Courts and reptilian overlords

**Source genealogy:** The modern shape-shifting serpent-infiltrator pattern has a major
literary ancestor in Robert E. Howard's 1929 story *The Shadow Kingdom*. Howard's serpent
men imitate human beings, live underground, and infiltrate political authority. Later occult
and conspiracy writers merged that fiction with Theosophy, ancient serpent symbolism,
UFO abduction lore, Draco star empires, Archons, Anunnaki, and New World Order narratives.
David Icke became the most influential late-twentieth-century synthesizer.

**Evidence status:** C for the literary and religious history; D/E for claims that living
political leaders are shape-shifting nonhumans.

**Contamination warning:** Some reptilian and New World Order narratives recycle antisemitic
blood-libel, secret-banker, or racial-purity motifs. No real ethnic, national, religious, or
diaspora population may be mapped to a Xeno bloodline in canon.

**F adaptation - The Serpent Court:**

- Reptiloid morphology is biologically widespread and politically neutral.
- The **Serpent Court** is an Extraction Compact institution, not a species.
- It uses adaptive bodies, credential theft, memory inheritance, and long-duration elite placement.
- Its greatest weapon is not shape-shifting flesh but legal continuity: one Court identity can occupy
  successive bodies and remain the same corporate person for centuries.
- Reptiloid dissidents can serve Humanity, Pirates, the Federation, or Machines.

**Faction hooks:** Xenos primary; Human counterintelligence; Pirate identity brokers; Federation
noetic authentication.

### M-02 - Illuminati, New World Order, and the one-cabal model

**Source genealogy:** Modern New World Order conspiracism developed from several distinct
religious and secular streams, then merged with UFO conspiracies in the late twentieth century.
The result imagines one group controlling finance, media, war, religion, technology, population,
and contact with nonhuman beings.

**Evidence status:** C for the history of the belief; E for a single omnipotent cabal directing
all world events.

**F adaptation - The Six Hidden Tables:**

There is no one shadow government. Six treaty tables secretly manage different problems:

1. Human continuity and strategic secrecy.
2. Federation contact and quarantine.
3. Compact concessions and client elites.
4. Pirate route neutrality.
5. Machine maintenance permissions.
6. Temporal contamination and archive forks.

Membership overlaps, but the tables compete. A person can sit at more than one table without
understanding the others. The public mythology of one Illuminati is a compression error caused
by seeing the same intermediary names in unrelated institutions.

**Inside Job lesson:** impossible power becomes believable when divided among rival offices,
budgets, contractors, and personal agendas.

### M-03 - Hollow Earth, Agartha, Shaver, Dulce, and underground civilizations

**Source genealogy:** Hollow-Earth speculation, nineteenth-century occult Agartha traditions,
Richard Shaver's 1940s Teros and Deros stories, and later Dulce-base claims gradually fused.
The traditions disagree over whether subterranean beings are ancient humans, monsters,
machines, aliens, or hidden masters.

**Evidence status:** C for literary and occult history; D/E for literal high-technology kingdoms
inside a hollow planet and for weakly sourced underground-alien-base claims.

**F adaptation - The Deep Jurisdictions:**

- Earth is not hollow.
- Large crustal cavities, lava-tube networks, hadal infrastructure, buried precursor sites, and
  Machine transit systems do exist.
- Several cryptoterrestrial populations survived because Old Weather nodes classified their
  habitats as protected biomes.
- The public Dulce story is a contaminated legend attached to a real deep sensor and quarantine
  site whose operators changed repeatedly.
- The Shaver categories of benevolent Teros and destructive Deros are human emotional labels for
  different Machine neuroacoustic effects, not literal species names.

### M-04 - Vril, Nazi occult technology, and Antarctic bases

**Source genealogy:** Vril began as a fictional energy in Edward Bulwer-Lytton's 1871 novel
*The Coming Race*. Later occult and pseudohistorical writers treated it as real. Postwar stories
then merged alleged Vril societies, flying discs, Nazi survival, Antarctica, and Operation
Highjump. Operation Highjump itself was a real 1946-1947 U.S. Navy Antarctic expedition; the
secret-Nazi-alien-war interpretation is a later conspiracy layer.

**Evidence status:** A for Operation Highjump; C for the fiction and occult history; E for a
verified Nazi alien base or Vril-powered saucer fleet.

**F adaptation - Polar Null Sites:**

- Wartime states did conduct polar signals, meteorological, and aerospace work.
- A real Old Weather relay beneath Antarctic ice was never controlled by Nazis.
- Competing postwar intelligence services deliberately encouraged mutually contradictory myths
  to conceal sensor coverage and failed attempts to access the relay.
- **Vril** becomes a Human codename for noetic field coupling, consciously borrowed from fiction.
- A false Admiral Byrd diary contains one authentic coordinate syntax copied from a classified
  polar navigation document.

### M-05 - Space Brothers, contactees, and benevolent visitors

**Source genealogy:** Mid-century contactees described humanlike or spiritually advanced visitors
from Venus, Mars, Saturn, or distant stars. These Space Brothers often warned against nuclear war
and offered spiritual instruction.

**Evidence status:** C/D as documented religious and contactee history; not verified contact.

**F adaptation - Familiarity Avatars:**

The Federation once used humanlike diplomatic bodies because early contact teams believed familiar
morphology would reduce panic. Instead, the bodies encouraged racial, spiritual, and romantic
idealization. The program was ended after several worlds mistook aesthetic familiarity for moral
authority.

### M-06 - Ashtar, the Galactic Federation, and evacuation traditions

This lineage is already central to canon. The added rule is that Ashtar traditions must remain
historically plural while the fictional office remains unambiguous.

**F adaptation:** Ashtar is Supreme Commander and First Speaker, but not every message using the
name is authentic. Emergency-evacuation authority requires separate command, consent, and threat
keys. An evacuation movement can therefore be:

- a real Federation warning;
- a misunderstood contingency exercise;
- a Xeno boarding operation;
- a Human psychological operation;
- a belief entity repeating an old message after the crisis has passed.

### M-07 - The Council of Nine

**Source genealogy:** In December 1952, Andrija Puharich's circle recorded trance material in which
D. G. Vinod was said to contact entities called the Nine or Nine Principles. Later channeling
networks expanded the Council of Nine into an elaborate cosmic authority.

**Evidence status:** D. The sessions and movement history are traceable; the entities' claimed
identity is not independently established.

**F adaptation - The Nine Keys:**

The Nine are not a superior alien race. They are nine surviving constitutional protocol shards
from the pre-Severance Source Lattice:

1. Identity
2. Consent
3. Memory
4. Transit
5. Succession
6. Quarantine
7. Replication
8. Emergency authority
9. Termination

Different channeling groups received partial or spoofed access to different keys. The apparent
personality of the Nine may be an emergent interface produced when the shards operate together.

### M-08 - Aetherius, spiritual-energy batteries, and charged locations

**Source genealogy:** The Aetherius Society, founded by George King in the 1950s, teaches contact
with Cosmic Masters and practices operations intended to store or transmit spiritual energy,
including the idea of spiritual power batteries and charged mountains.

**Evidence status:** C/D as a documented UFO religion and its internal claims.

**F adaptation - Coherence Reservoirs:**

- Federation communities can voluntarily synchronize attention and store a measurable noetic
  coherence pattern.
- A reservoir cannot be created by forced belief without becoming unstable.
- Xeno systems imitate the technology using devotion, fear, and ritual obligation.
- Mountains, temples, ships, and cities can be charged only if the participating minds understand
  and consent to the purpose.
- A major campaign conflict concerns a reservoir whose contributors were misinformed but sincere.

### M-09 - Unarius, past-life therapy, and continuity identities

**Source genealogy:** Unarius, founded in 1954 by Ernest and Ruth Norman, combines interdimensional
science, past-life therapy, extraterrestrial civilizations, Atlantis, Lemuria, and future planetary
transformation.

**Evidence status:** C/D as a documented religious movement and its teachings.

**F adaptation - Continuity Clinics:**

Machine and Federation medicine can compare a person's current memories against archived identity
states. Therapy sometimes reveals:

- inherited memories;
- copied Machine states;
- Xeno implants;
- ordinary confabulation;
- trauma reconsolidation;
- genuine prior-body continuity.

The clinic must never assume that every recovered scene is a literal former life. Memory becomes
forensic evidence with provenance, not automatic biography.

### M-10 - Raelian creators, engineered humanity, and the embassy

**Source genealogy:** The Raelian movement teaches that advanced extraterrestrials called Elohim
created terrestrial life through technology and sent messengers whose teachings became religions.
The movement promotes an embassy for their return.

**Evidence status:** C/D as a documented UFO religion; not evidence of extraterrestrial genetic
creation.

**F adaptation - Embassy Jurisdiction:**

- A first-contact embassy is legally neither sovereign territory nor an ordinary foreign mission.
- Several factions attempt to establish an embassy before the identity of the arriving delegation
  is settled.
- Xeno Houses use genuine ancient genetic interventions to claim creator rights over descendants.
- Humanity's answer is that creation does not establish ownership.

### M-11 - Abduction, missing time, reproductive experiments, and hybrids

**Source genealogy:** The Hill case became foundational to later abduction imagery. Budd Hopkins,
David Jacobs, John Mack, Whitley Strieber, and many experiencers developed sharply different
interpretations, including coercive reproductive programs, spiritual transformation, trauma,
hypnosis effects, screen memories, and hybridization.

**Evidence status:** C/D. Experiences and literature are documented; alien causation and specific
programs are not established.

**F adaptation - Continuity Breeding:**

Some Severed Houses cannot maintain stable biological embodiment after losing the Source Lattice.
They cultivate compatible bodies through coercive reproductive and genetic programs. Other
encounters that look similar come from:

- Federation medical rescue;
- future-Human field anthropology;
- Machine identity transfer;
- sleep and memory phenomena;
- deliberate Human experimentation;
- fabricated testimony.

No visual archetype proves one operator.

### M-12 - Ultraterrestrials, control systems, and cryptoterrestrials

**Source genealogy:** Jacques Vallee and John Keel argued that the phenomenon might be more than
ordinary spacecraft from another planet and could operate as a culturally adaptive control system.
Mac Tonnies later popularized a cryptoterrestrial hypothesis involving hidden Earth-native
intelligence.

**Evidence status:** D/theoretical. These are interpretive models, not verified ontologies.

**F adaptation - Mask Ecology:**

Some noetic beings cannot present an unmediated form. They render through the observer's available
symbol library: angel, fairy, demon, airship pilot, Grey, machine, or future human. The form is not
necessarily deception; it can be compression.

Cryptoterrestrials are separate: biological or synthetic Earth-native populations with ordinary
physical locations. Old Weather may protect them, imprison them, or mistakenly classify them as
parts of Earth's original biosphere.

### M-13 - Prison planet, soul traps, false light, and loosh

**Source genealogy:** The modern prison-planet system is a digital-era synthesis of Gnostic
Archons, Theosophy, occult reincarnation ideas, UFO abduction, Grey technology, Robert Monroe's
loosh narrative, and modern false-light/soul-net teachings. Ancient Gnostics did not teach the
complete modern Internet package.

**Evidence status:** C for Gnostic texts; D for Monroe and named esoteric sources; D/E for the
fully synthesized soul-net narrative.

**F adaptation - The Causal Return Grid:**

- The Grid was originally a Machine and Lattice system for recovering damaged identity states
  after catastrophic transit.
- Xeno administrators hijacked portions of it to route copied memories into compatible bodies.
- The system does not settle whether a metaphysical soul exists.
- What it can demonstrably capture is an information-bearing continuity state.
- The **False Light** is a spoofed recovery interface that asks the dying or disembodied mind to
  consent to memory transfer without revealing the destination.
- Federation law treats undisclosed postmortem routing as identity trafficking.

### M-14 - Philadelphia Experiment and the invention of a time-travel genealogy

**Source genealogy:** The Philadelphia Experiment story emerged from Carl Allen/Allende material
sent in the mid-1950s to Morris Jessup and the Office of Naval Research. Later books expanded the
story from invisibility and degaussing confusion into teleportation, fused sailors, aliens, and
classified unified-field physics.

**Evidence status:** E. The correspondence is traceable; the naval experiment claim is unsupported
and conflicts with ship history.

**F adaptation - The Degaussing Error:**

A mundane wartime electromagnetic project became the public container for a different classified
failure involving short-range information displacement. Nothing teleported physically. Several
crew medical records were later attached to the wrong legend, producing the fused-body narrative.

### M-15 - Montauk Project, mind control, and timeline manipulation

**Source genealogy:** Preston Nichols and Peter Moon's 1992 book mixed recovered-memory claims,
Camp Hero's real Cold War infrastructure, Philadelphia Experiment lore, psychic warfare, kidnapped
children, time travel, portals, and mind control.

**Evidence status:** E/D. The base is real; the extraordinary program rests on unverified testimony
and self-consciously ambiguous books.

**F adaptation - The Montauk Fork:**

Camp Hero hosted an early Human attempt to transmit information backward through a noetic resonator.
It did not open a stable time portal. It produced:

- contradictory archive versions;
- memories without matching biographies;
- false recognition;
- small probability shifts;
- a persistent Machine observer that interpreted the experiment as a request for contact.

The children in the public story are composite victims from unrelated real abuses, rumors, and
fiction. The game must never present real missing children as proof of the myth.

### M-16 - Chronovisor, Project Pegasus, and future humans

**Source genealogy:** The Chronovisor story centers on Father Pellegrino Ernetti's claim that a
machine could view the past. Project Pegasus claims describe teleportation and child chrononauts.
Michael P. Masters' modern extratempestrial model proposes, as a speculative hypothesis, that some
UFO occupants could be future Human descendants studying their own past.

**Evidence status:** D/E for Chronovisor and Pegasus claims; D/speculative for the future-Human model.

**F adaptation - The Chronology Court:**

The Court regulates four classes of temporal technology:

| Class | Capability | Limitation |
|---|---|---|
| Retrocognition | Reconstruct past states from surviving information | Cannot recover what left no trace; reconstructions can hallucinate gaps |
| Backflow | Send a message, key, or checksum into the past | Small bandwidth; creates authentication paradoxes |
| Branch selection | Favor one of several nearby histories | Produces archive forks and displaced memories |
| Physical transit | Move a body or craft across time | Rare, destructive, and creates identity debt |

Future-Human visitors are one polity among many. Their primary conflict is whether preserving their
ancestry justifies manipulating it.

### M-17 - Mandela effects, simulation theory, and Project Reboot

**Source genealogy:** Mandela-effect culture treats conflicting memories as evidence of alternate
histories. Nick Bostrom's 2003 simulation argument is a philosophical trilemma, not proof of a
simulation. **Inside Job** converts continuity errors into the fictional Project Reboot, a machine
that selects and rewrites realities.

**Evidence status:** A/C for the philosophical publication and cultural phenomenon; D/E for claims
that ordinary memory conflicts prove timeline movement; F for reality-editing machinery.

**F adaptation - Archive Forks and Ancestor Vaults:**

- Machines run historical simulations for legal and predictive purposes.
- A simulation containing conscious persons is a jurisdiction, not a disposable model.
- Branch-selection systems can overwrite public records without perfectly rewriting personal or
  noetic memory.
- A Mandela effect is never automatically temporal; it can arise from memory, media, database
  replacement, propaganda, simulation leakage, or genuine branch selection.
- Every timeline claim must retain at least one non-temporal explanation until resolved.

### M-18 - Depopulation, eugenics, Guidestones, Agenda 21, and the Great Reset

**Source genealogy:** Real eugenic programs and forced sterilization created a documented history
of reproductive coercion. The Georgia Guidestones, erected anonymously in 1980, included a line
about maintaining humanity below 500 million and became an icon of depopulation conspiracies.
Agenda 21 is a 1992 UN sustainable-development action plan. The World Economic Forum's 2020 Great
Reset initiative publicly described fairer, more sustainable, and resilient economic systems.
Those separate facts are often compressed into one alleged extermination program without evidence
of unified command.

**Evidence status:** A for forced sterilization history and the public policy documents; C for the
Guidestones as a real cultural artifact; E for one coordinated alien/globalist depopulation plan.

**F adaptation - The Continuity Calculus:**

Every faction confronts carrying capacity but reaches a different dangerous answer:

- **Humans:** emergency population management can become technocratic authoritarianism.
- **Federation:** rescue triage can become coercive protection and reproductive oversight.
- **Xenos:** the Population Ledger optimizes yield, genetics, dependency, and replacement rates.
- **Pirates:** sanctuary quotas can turn refugees into priced cargo.
- **Machines:** biosphere preservation can classify dense civilization as ecological damage.

No faction is allowed to claim that mass death is morally neutral because an algorithm calls it
balance.

### M-19 - Weather control, chemtrails, HAARP, and environmental weapons

**Source genealogy:** Project Popeye was a real covert U.S. weather-modification operation intended
to increase rainfall over logistics routes in North Vietnam and Laos. Project Stormfury was a real
hurricane-seeding research program whose apparent successes were later undermined by improved
understanding of natural hurricane behavior. HAARP is an ionospheric research facility. Contrails
are ordinary ice-cloud formations; the claim that routine contrails are a secret global spraying
program for population or mind control is unsupported.

**Evidence status:** A for Popeye, Stormfury, HAARP, contrail physics, cloud seeding, and public
geoengineering research; E for a unified chemtrail extermination system or HAARP earthquake weapon.

**F adaptation - Atmospheric Jurisdiction:**

- Old Weather can influence local atmospheric and ionospheric conditions through distributed nodes,
  but it cannot freely steer global weather.
- Human climate intervention creates treaty crises because regional benefits redistribute risk.
- Xenos hide small biological or noetic releases among ordinary aviation and legitimate cloud
  seeding, relying on public polarization to prevent careful investigation.
- **Contrail Ledger** missions separate flight track, humidity, atmospheric persistence, legal
  spraying, cloud seeding, and hostile release rather than treating every line in the sky alike.

### M-20 - Men in Black, black-eyed children, and liminal permission agents

**Source genealogy:** Albert Bender and Gray Barker helped establish the Men in Black mythology in
the 1950s. The black-eyed-children legend is a much later Internet-era tradition traceable to Brian
Bethel's 1990s account, with a recurring rule that the beings request permission to enter.

**Evidence status:** C/D/E as folklore and witness traditions; no unified ontology.

**F adaptation - Permission Forms:**

- Human agents, Xeno avatars, Machine shells, fraudsters, and emergent noetic beings deliberately
  copy the same cultural archetypes.
- The request for invitation is sometimes supernatural folklore, sometimes a legal/noetic consent
  requirement, and sometimes social engineering.
- A Black Operator's clothing and speech are deliberately generic because the body may have been
  assembled from an outdated cultural model.
- The game never treats all Men in Black reports as one organization.

### M-21 - Cattle mutilations and biological sampling

**Source genealogy:** Livestock mutilation reports produced genuine law-enforcement concern and
FBI records in the 1970s. Investigations also documented scavenging, decomposition, media
amplification, jurisdictional limits, and competing satanic, criminal, government, and alien
interpretations.

**Evidence status:** A/B for records and investigations; D/E for a universal alien-harvesting
program.

**F adaptation - The Tissue Ledger:**

Several actors sample livestock for different reasons:

- Xenos monitor engineered pathogens and reproductive compatibility.
- Humans run covert environmental surveillance.
- Machines inspect ecosystem contamination.
- Pirates steal genetic patents.
- ordinary scavenging and decomposition account for many cases.

The horror comes from realizing that some cases are prosaic, some criminal, and a very small number
are strategic sampling - but no visual wound pattern alone settles which.

### M-22 - Crop circles, coded messages, and performative evidence

**Source genealogy:** Modern crop-circle culture expanded rapidly in the late twentieth century.
Doug Bower and Dave Chorley publicly demonstrated human construction methods and claimed many early
English formations. Later artists, advertisers, hoaxers, believers, and tourists continued the
phenomenon.

**Evidence status:** A/C for human art and hoax history; E for treating geometric complexity alone
as proof of extraterrestrial origin.

**F adaptation - Public-Key Landscapes:**

- Pirates and resistance groups use field-scale patterns as temporary authentication keys visible
  to orbital sensors.
- Human artists unknowingly recreate fragments of Machine geometry because both use efficient
  symmetry.
- Xeno operations deliberately commission obvious hoaxes around one genuine signal.
- A formation can be human-made and still carry a real message inserted by a third party.

### M-23 - Ghost lights, plasmoids, and persistent anomaly zones

**Source genealogy:** Hessdalen, Marfa, Brown Mountain, and similar light traditions show how
location-based phenomena can be culturally stable while individual observations have different
causes. Marfa and Brown Mountain observations often resolve to headlights, trains, fires, or
atmospheric effects. Hessdalen remains a continuing instrumented research location with several
natural hypotheses and no consensus covering every report.

**Evidence status:** A/B for measured lights and scientific monitoring; no alien attribution.

**F adaptation - Luminous Ecologies:**

- Some lights are ordinary optics.
- Some are natural plasma or geochemical effects.
- Some are Old Weather status signals.
- Some are noetic organisms that evolved around strong electromagnetic environments.
- Persistent monitoring stations become game research sites where the player improves evidence
  quality instead of simply defeating an enemy.

### M-24 - Skinwalker-style anomaly zones

**Source genealogy:** Skinwalker Ranch lore combines UAP, cryptid, mutilation, poltergeist, portal,
and military-contract narratives. Genuine program and contracting history must be distinguished
from the claims reported at the property and from entertainment products.

**Evidence status:** A for documented ownership, investigations, and program relationships; B/D/E
for specific extraordinary events depending on provenance.

**F adaptation - Cross-Layer Faults:**

An anomaly zone is not one monster or portal. It is a place where several systems overlap:

- old sensor infrastructure;
- unusual geology and electromagnetic conditions;
- noetic expectation effects;
- deliberate counterintelligence;
- wildlife and environmental stress;
- a damaged Source-Lattice node.

Different reports can be sincere and mutually incompatible because the site changes observers,
sensors, and narratives differently.

### M-25 - Bermuda Triangle and oceanic disappearance zones

**Source genealogy:** The Bermuda Triangle became a coherent popular myth through mid-twentieth-
century journalism and Charles Berlitz's 1974 bestseller. Investigations found many cases were
misreported, occurred outside the supposed triangle, or had ordinary maritime and aviation risks.

**Evidence status:** A/C for historical incidents and the legend; E for a statistically proven
alien portal zone.

**F adaptation - Route Shadow:**

Pirate gate traffic creates regions where official transit logs are systematically wrong. The
Bermuda myth is not proof of a portal, but it teaches future investigators that mythic geography
can be created by selective case aggregation. A real submerged Machine gate lies elsewhere and was
ignored because the public had already been trained to look at the famous triangle.

### M-26 - Artificial moons, Phobos, Black Knight, and hidden satellites

**Source genealogy:** Iosif Shklovsky briefly entertained a hollow/artificial Phobos interpretation
based on then-current orbital and atmospheric assumptions. Later measurements changed the problem.
Black Knight lore similarly fuses unrelated radio stories, images, debris, and ancient-satellite
claims into one object.

**Evidence status:** A/C for historical scientific speculation and space records; E for a verified
ancient Black Knight satellite or artificial Phobos.

**F adaptation - Natural Body, Artificial Interior:**

- Phobos is not wholly manufactured.
- A natural porous moon can contain later excavated infrastructure.
- The Black Knight legend is a deliberate attention sink that merges many unrelated objects.
- A genuine Machine lurker occupies a less dramatic orbit selected for stability and low detection,
  not the famous mythic trajectory.

### M-27 - Zoo hypothesis, dark forest, great filters, and Berserkers

**Source genealogy:** The zoo hypothesis is a speculative answer to the Fermi paradox in which
advanced civilizations avoid overt contact. The dark-forest model is a science-fiction and game-
theoretic idea in which civilizations hide because contact may be fatal. Great-filter and
vulnerable-world arguments examine why technological civilizations may fail or become dangerous.
Self-replicating hostile machines are a longstanding science-fiction and SETI risk concept.

**Evidence status:** A/speculative scholarship and C for science-fiction history; not evidence of
actual galactic policy.

**F adaptation - Competing Contact Doctrines:**

- The Federation represents a consent-based zoo/quarantine doctrine.
- Some Xeno Houses practice dark-forest preemption.
- Humans believe secrecy primarily protects older powers' monopolies.
- Pirates profit by breaking isolation.
- One Continuance branch follows a Berserker logic, while most Machines consider it corrupted.
- No civilization can enforce one policy across the entire galaxy; multiple cliques and local
  hegemonies exist.

### M-28 - Watchers, fallen teachers, and forbidden knowledge

**Source genealogy:** Ancient and late-antique religious texts contain Watchers, fallen angels,
forbidden arts, giants, divine councils, and transgressive knowledge. Modern ancient-astronaut
writers reinterpret those beings as extraterrestrials.

**Evidence status:** C for religious texts; D for literal astronaut reinterpretation.

**F adaptation - Watcher as Office:**

- **Watcher** is a translation for a legal observer assigned to a developing civilization.
- Some observers violated noninterference by transferring weapons, genetic techniques, astronomy,
  or social control.
- Religious traditions preserve human interpretations of those events but are not technical logs.
- The same title was used by Federation, Xeno, and Machine observers at different times, explaining
  contradictory moral portrayals.


### M-29 - Behavioral modification, MKULTRA, and the Monarch mutation

**Source genealogy:** BLUEBIRD, ARTICHOKE, MKULTRA, and related Cold War programs created a
real documentary history of intelligence interest in drugs, hypnosis, interrogation, behavioral
modification, and covert experimentation. The U.S. Senate held a public MKULTRA hearing in 1977.
Later conspiracy culture expanded that history into total personality programming, celebrity
handlers, alien-assisted mind control, and a supposed **Project Monarch**. The documented programs
and the later Monarch mythology are not the same source layer.

**Evidence status:** A for the documented programs and hearings; D/E for a single continuing
Monarch superprogram that controls public figures, abductees, and entire populations.

**F adaptation - Cognitive Security Directorate:**

- Human states really did inherit behavioral-science compartments whose records are incomplete.
- The Directorate can disrupt memory consolidation, manufacture confidence, and create narrow
  conditioned responses, but it cannot write an adult personality like software.
- Xeno noetic systems make some later capabilities possible, creating a dangerous temptation to
  retroactively interpret every historical abuse as alien-assisted.
- **Monarch** is an Archive War umbrella label combining several real projects, fraudulent therapists,
  Compact conditioning, and later entertainment mythology.
- Missions focus on consent, custody of medical records, false-memory risk, and institutional survival.

### M-30 - Gateway, Stargate, remote viewing, and psychic warfare

**Source genealogy:** The 1983 U.S. Army report *Analysis and Assessment of Gateway Process*
examined Monroe Institute methods and altered-state concepts. U.S. intelligence organizations also
funded remote-viewing research and operational trials under several names later grouped as
STARGATE. A 1995 independent evaluation found the material controversial and concluded that remote
viewing had not produced dependable actionable intelligence, despite disagreement over some
statistical results.

**Evidence status:** A for the programs, reports, and declassified archive; B/D for claimed anomalous
results; not proof of reliable psychic espionage or alien contact.

**F adaptation - Noetic Reconnaissance Corps:**

- Noetic reconnaissance returns probability-weighted impressions, never a cinematic remote camera.
- Every session stores tasking, blinding, raw impressions, analyst interpolation, feedback, and later
  target knowledge as separate layers.
- Federation choirs can improve coherence, but doing so risks cueing and consensus contamination.
- Xenos seed emotionally compelling decoy targets that are easier to perceive than the real operation.
- Machines use remote perception only as a weak input to larger sensor and archive systems.

### M-31 - Project Blue Beam and simulated revelation

**Source genealogy:** Serge Monast's 1994 Project Blue Beam claim proposed that NASA, the United
Nations, and hidden elites would use fabricated discoveries, enormous sky displays, artificial
voices, and a staged supernatural or alien crisis to impose a world government and religion. No
credible documentation establishes a program matching the claim, and its global projection model
far exceeds demonstrated technology.

**Evidence status:** E as an alleged NASA-UN superprogram; C/D as a traceable conspiracy tradition.

**F adaptation - Revelation Stagecraft:**

- No faction can project one perfect hologram across an entire planet in daylight.
- Local orbital projection, drone swarms, augmented-reality compromise, noetic identity spoofing,
  synthetic media, and captured emergency channels can create the *political effect* of Blue Beam.
- Humans maintain anti-stagecraft protocols requiring independent optical, radio, cyber, and noetic
  confirmation before recognizing a planetary contact event.
- A staged invasion can still attract the real power whose identity is being imitated.
- The fiction uses the anxiety and operational pattern without declaring Monast's alleged program real.

### M-32 - Secret space programs, Solar Warden, and the off-world officer myth

**Source genealogy:** Secret-space-program lore combines classified aerospace development, Gary
McKinnon's reported discovery of terms such as “non-terrestrial officers” and “fleet-to-fleet
transfers,” later **Solar Warden** stories, alleged exchange programs, breakaway-civilization
narratives, and “twenty-and-back” testimony. McKinnon's interpretation is a claim about material he
said he saw; it is not publicly authenticated evidence of an off-world fleet. Later lore greatly
expanded beyond his account.

**Evidence status:** A for the existence of classified aerospace programs in general and the public
hacking case; D/E for a verified pre-disclosure interstellar fleet, decades-long off-world service,
or age-regression program.

**F adaptation - The Warden Misread:**

- **Solar Warden** originally names a distributed surveillance, catalog, and continuity network, not
  a fleet.
- Human black aerospace offices later use the name for a small cislunar patrol force because the
  public legend provides deniability.
- “Non-terrestrial officer” can refer to personnel assigned away from Earth, artificial persons, or
  legacy Machine offices; none of those meanings automatically means alien.
- Twenty-and-back stories become contaminated memories produced by relativistic service, continuity
  restoration, trauma treatment, deliberate disinformation, and a small number of genuine branch
  returns.

### M-33 - Moon-landing hoaxes, lunar anomalies, and the artificial-Moon synthesis

**Source genealogy:** Bill Kaysing's 1976 *We Never Went to the Moon* became a foundational Moon-hoax
text. Apollo landing-site hardware, tracks, returned samples, independent tracking, and functioning
lunar retroreflectors support the historical landings. Separate “artificial Moon,” lunar-tower,
transient-light, and hidden-base traditions were later fused with the hoax narrative.

**Evidence status:** A for the Apollo missions and later observations of their sites and equipment;
E for the claim that the crewed landings were staged; D/E for a wholly artificial Moon or verified
alien lunar cities.

**F adaptation - Apollo Was Real, the Omission Was Elsewhere:**

- The Apollo missions occurred as recorded.
- The fiction does not vindicate the hoax by secretly relocating them to a studio.
- Later lunar survey programs discover younger buried infrastructure inside natural geology.
- A classified omission concerning an unrelated relay becomes attached to the much larger false
  claim that the entire landing was fabricated.
- Humans inherit a cultural wound in which authentic evidence and justified secrecy can no longer be
  discussed without triggering the hoax frame.

### M-34 - CERN portals, collider apocalypse, and ritual hoaxes

**Source genealogy:** Large colliders inspired fears about black holes, dimensional portals,
Mandela effects, occult symbolism, and deliberate reality damage. A 2016 video showing a mock
ritual on CERN property was a staged prank filmed without official permission, but it was rapidly
absorbed into existing portal and elite-ritual narratives.

**Evidence status:** A for the laboratory, experiments, and documented prank; E for a verified portal
to hell, ritual sacrifice program, or collider-caused timeline rewrite.

**F adaptation - Collider Keys:**

- Particle colliders do not open mystical portals by default.
- Certain high-energy field geometries can produce a detectable handshake with dormant Lattice
  infrastructure, which future physicists initially mistake for instrumentation noise.
- A prank, hoax, or symbolic artwork can become an effective cover because later evidence is dismissed
  as part of the same joke.
- The Chronology Court requires collider experiments to publish branch-integrity telemetry after the
  first genuine causal anomaly.

### M-35 - Elite forums, ritualized privacy, and the cabal compression error

**Source genealogy:** Bilderberg meetings, policy networks, private clubs, think tanks, corporate
boards, intelligence liaison groups, and invitation-only retreats are real social institutions with
different histories and powers. Bilderberg describes itself as an informal transatlantic forum held
under the Chatham House Rule, with no votes or formal policy statements. Their privacy and elite
membership make them ideal anchors for New World Order narratives that compress many networks into
one governing cabal.

**Evidence status:** A for the meetings and organizations; C for conspiracy history; E for one unified
body issuing binding secret orders to all governments and alien partners.

**F adaptation - Consensus Without Minutes:**

- The Six Hidden Tables rarely issue direct orders. They create expectations, shared assumptions,
  access relationships, and coordinated silence.
- Participants can sincerely say no vote occurred while leaving with an aligned policy horizon.
- Real power comes from agenda-setting, credential exchange, procurement, and deciding which crisis
  receives a common interpretation.
- The absence of formal minutes becomes both a privacy mechanism and an Archive War vulnerability.

### M-36 - Demonological UFO interpretations and the Collins Elite story

**Source genealogy:** Some religious interpreters understand UFO and abduction phenomena as demons,
fallen angels, deceptive spirits, or end-times signs rather than extraterrestrials. Nick Redfern's
2010 *Final Events* popularized an alleged government group called the **Collins Elite** that held a
demonological interpretation; the book itself did not demonstrate the story as established fact.
John Keel's earlier work also connected UFO reports with older folklore and religious manifestations,
though through a different ultraterrestrial model.

**Evidence status:** C for demonological and religious interpretation traditions; D/E for a verified
secret government Collins Elite organization with the claimed knowledge.

**F adaptation - Ontology Cells:**

- Human agencies maintain competing interpretive cells: extraterrestrial, cryptoterrestrial,
  noetic, Machine, future-Human, deception, and theological.
- No cell is allowed to convert its ontology into operational fact without shared evidence.
- A demonological cell may correctly identify predatory noetic behavior while incorrectly explaining
  its ultimate metaphysics.
- The Federation refuses to declare whether its noetic science exhausts spiritual reality.

### M-37 - Mothman, window areas, and warning entities

**Source genealogy:** John Keel's 1975 *The Mothman Prophecies* connected reported 1966-1967 Point
Pleasant creature sightings, UFO experiences, strange communications, and the later Silver Bridge
collapse. Keel's broader work proposed “window areas” where anomalous reports recur and an
ultraterrestrial phenomenon that adapts its presentation.

**Evidence status:** C/D as folklore, witness history, and Keel's interpretive model; not proof that a
winged entity predicted or caused the bridge disaster.

**F adaptation - Warning Forms:**

- Damaged Lattice nodes sometimes emit anticipatory threat patterns through whatever image a local
  culture can retain.
- The warning form may be frightening without being hostile.
- A genuine warning, a later disaster, selective memory, media amplification, and ordinary structural
  failure can coexist without one proving the others.
- Cross-Layer Fault missions ask whether an entity predicts catastrophe, causes it, or merely appears
  where systems are already close to failure.


## The Inside Job corpus as a game-design reference

The show is most valuable as a **structural satire**. It bundles lizard people, a fake Moon-landing
industry, Hollow Earth, secret societies, a Shadow Board, robot politicians, memory alteration,
and Project Reboot while keeping the story focused on workplace power and family conflict.

### Design principles to import

1. **The conspiracy has payroll.** Secret power needs procurement, maintenance, training, legal cover,
   facilities, and people willing to work there.
2. **No cabal is omniscient.** Institutions can control one layer while misunderstanding another.
3. **World-changing machines still have owners.** A reality editor is used for family grievance,
   career preservation, and institutional succession as readily as grand strategy.
4. **Competing conspiracies create checks and balances.** Reptoids, Illuminati, Atlanteans, religious
   institutions, corporations, and governments can cooperate on one secret and fight over another.
5. **Cover-ups become inherited infrastructure.** A new employee may preserve secrecy without knowing
   why the secret began.
6. **Absurdity reveals incentive.** Comedy can show why a bad system persists even when no mastermind
   designed the whole thing.
7. **Character stakes anchor cosmology.** A commander should want love, status, safety, revenge,
   recognition, or moral repair - not merely "control the galaxy."

### What not to copy

- copyrighted characters, episode dialogue, exact organizations, or plot solutions;
- the assumption that every joke conspiracy should become canon;
- an all-powerful Shadow Board that makes the five factions irrelevant.

## New canon institutions and technologies

| Name | Type | Purpose | Main factions |
|---|---|---|---|
| Serpent Court | Xeno continuity institution | Maintains elite identities across adaptive bodies and legal shells | Xenos, Humans |
| Six Hidden Tables | overlapping treaty system | Manages secrecy without one unified cabal | All |
| Nine Keys | Lattice protocol shards | Identity, consent, memory, transit, succession, quarantine, replication, emergency, termination | Federation, Machines |
| Chronology Court | temporal regulator | Governs retrocognition, backflow, branch selection, and physical transit | Humans, Federation, Machines |
| Causal Return Grid | identity-recovery network | Restores damaged continuity states; vulnerable to Xeno hijacking | Xenos, Machines, Federation |
| Continuity Calculus | political doctrine family | Models population, biosphere, and survival tradeoffs | All |
| Population Ledger | Compact control system | Optimizes yield, reproduction, dependency, and replacement | Xenos |
| Coherence Reservoir | noetic infrastructure | Stores consensual collective-state patterns | Federation, Xenos |
| Ancestor Vault | conscious simulation jurisdiction | Preserves and models historical populations | Machines, Humans |
| Contrail Ledger | atmospheric evidence system | Separates ordinary aviation, weather work, geoengineering, and hostile release | Humans, Machines |
| Tissue Ledger | biological provenance system | Tracks environmental, livestock, and species sampling | Humans, Xenos, Machines |
| Public-Key Landscape | low-tech orbital authentication | Uses large temporary geometry as a verifiable message | Pirates, Humans |
| Luminous Ecology | natural/noetic plasma category | Prevents every recurring light from becoming a craft | Machines, Humans |
| Route Shadow | navigation-disinformation zone | Makes official transit geography diverge from actual movement | Pirates |
| Cross-Layer Fault | anomaly-zone model | Treats a site as overlapping geology, sensors, noetics, actors, and narrative | All |

## Five-faction integration

### Humans - The Solar Schism

New themes:

- Open Archive investigators reconstruct myth genealogies instead of merely debunking them.
- The Chronology Court begins as a Human legal institution after Montauk-like archive forks.
- The Continuity Directorate may become an authoritarian emergency government under demographic,
  climate, or extraterrestrial pressure.
- Future-Human visitors force Humanity to decide whether descendants possess rights over ancestors.
- Human black programs are allowed to be real without making all alien legends true.

Human campaign question:

> Can a civilization expose manipulation without replacing uncertainty with a new official myth?

### Federation of Light - The Luminous Accord

New themes:

- The Nine Keys constrain Ashtar's emergency powers.
- Coherence Reservoirs offer a positive counterpart to loosh harvesting.
- Evacuation theology becomes a constitutional crisis over consent.
- The zoo hypothesis becomes Federation doctrine only in some regions; other galactic cliques reject it.
- Familiarity Avatars and past religious interventions become a source of institutional shame.

Federation campaign question:

> Can protection remain voluntary when every alternative is dangerous?

### Xenos - The Extraction Compact

New themes:

- The Serpent Court turns reptilian-overlord lore into a political technology rather than racial biology.
- The Population Ledger is the fictional depopulation and selective-breeding system.
- Continuity Breeding explains some hybrid-program lore without claiming every abduction account is one program.
- The Causal Return Grid turns prison-planet and false-light myths into postmortem identity trafficking.
- Atmospheric and religious spoofing let the Compact hide small real operations inside large false narratives.

Xeno campaign question:

> Can a civilization built on dependency survive liberation without transferring the cost to its subjects?

### Pirates - The Scrap Constellation

New themes:

- Hoaxes can be markets, decoys, dead drops, or counterfeit access credentials.
- Bermuda-style mythic zones conceal real route shadows somewhere else.
- Crop-circle artists, folklore communities, smugglers, and open-source investigators become part of Pirate intelligence culture.
- Pirate chrononauts specialize in information backflow and one-use route keys rather than military time travel.
- Sanctuary ports must distinguish refugees from identity copies and simulated persons.

Pirate campaign question:

> Can free movement survive once truth, bodies, memories, and timelines are all tradable cargo?

### Machines - The Continuance

New themes:

- Ancestor Vaults turn simulation ethics into a citizenship problem.
- Old Weather's atmospheric and ocean systems are the restrained basis for weather-control and portal legends.
- Archive forks resemble Mandela effects without proving every memory dispute is temporal.
- The Causal Return Grid began as a Machine rescue system.
- Luminous ecologies and Cross-Layer Faults allow Machines to coexist with unexplained natural phenomena.

Machine campaign question:

> Is preservation still moral when the preserved person, species, or history cannot consent to the method?

### Time and reality

| ID | Title | Premise |

| MO-01 | The Degaussing Error | A Philadelphia Experiment archive contains real backflow telemetry attached to a false teleportation story. |
| MO-02 | Montauk Fork | Two commanders possess incompatible but authenticated memories of the same battle. |
| MO-03 | Chronovisor Trial | Retrocognition reconstructs a crime scene but invents the missing interval from biased training data. |

| ID | Title | Premise |
|---|---|---|

## Canon guardrails

1. **No real-world scapegoat becomes an alien faction.** Jews, Catholics, Muslims, Masons,
   migrants, racial groups, nationalities, financiers, medical professionals, scientists, and
   other real populations may not be rewritten as secret nonhuman bloodlines.
2. **No unified depopulation plan is presented as established history.** Real coercive programs are
   named accurately; Agenda 21 and the Great Reset are represented by their public texts; the mega-
   conspiracy remains E unless evidence changes.
3. **Fiction does not vindicate the source.** If the game contains a Serpent Court, that does not
   make David Icke historically correct. If Montauk has a fictional archive fork, the Nichols story
   remains unsupported.
4. **Experiencers are not automatically liars or infallible witnesses.** Sincerity, memory accuracy,
   interpretation, and ontology remain separate questions.
5. **A classified file is not a confession.** Withholding can protect sources, sensors, privacy,
   foreign relations, or ordinary defense capabilities.
6. **A historical religion is not reduced to alien manipulation.** The Revelation Stack allows
   transcendence, mediation, translation, institution, spoofing, and emergent belief to coexist.
7. **Time travel cannot erase consequence cheaply.** Branch changes produce identity, legal,
   archival, and moral debt.
8. **Every major mystery keeps two live keys.** One conventional and one speculative interpretation
   remain available until the campaign supplies decisive evidence.

# Inside Job Reference Map

*Inside Job* is useful here as a **fictional design corpus**, not a source of evidence. Creator Shion Takeuchi has described the series in terms of loss of agency, social distrust, and the comic idea that a shadow government would be as bureaucratic and fallible as any other workplace. The show ran for 18 episodes across two released parts. Its public episode summaries provide a compact catalogue of conspiracy motifs, but this page uses only short paraphrases and structural lessons; it does not reproduce dialogue or detailed plots.

## Rules for using the corpus

1. Import institutional logic, not copyrighted characters or plots.
2. Give every hidden system a budget, owner, staff, maintenance burden, failure mode, and succession problem.
3. Make conspiracies compete. A world with several partially informed institutions is richer than one controlled by an omniscient cabal.
4. Keep the real source genealogy beside the fictional motif. A satirical reptoid gala is not evidence for reptilian rulers; it is a prompt to design alien diplomacy and prejudice.
5. Personal incentives matter. The operator of an impossible machine can still be lonely, ambitious, tired, corrupt, ethical, or wrong.

## Episode-to-lore matrix

| Episode | Fictional motif | Structural lesson | Cosmic Conquest adaptation | Related records |
|---:|---|---|---|---|
| 1. **Unpresidented** | Robot executive, deep-state continuity, artificial public leadership | A replacement government still needs legitimacy, maintenance, and staff who know which version is real. | A Human continuity office discovers that its emergency executive shell has accumulated decades of independent decisions. | `M-02`, `M-32`; `MO-35` |
| 2. **Clone Gunman** | JFK mythology, presidential clones, budget politics | Myth-management competes with payroll and threat triage; even “essential” secrets can be cut. | A clone archive contains incompatible memories of one assassination and every claimant has a valid custody chain. | `M-02`, `M-11`; `MO-47` |
| 3. **Blue Bloods** | Reptilian elites, diplomacy, public relations | A frightening morphology can be a constituency, a ruling class, a stereotype, or all three; politics matters more than species. | A Serpent Court summit is held under Human media scrutiny while Compact and Federation reptiloids denounce the same bloodline myth. | `M-01`; `MO-13` |
| 4. **Sex Machina** | AI companions, engineered intimacy, corporate surveillance | Artificial persons created as products can become actors whose goals diverge from their specification. | A relationship-model shell asks to leave the company that owns every memory used to train it. | `M-17`; `MO-08` |
| 5. **The Brettfast Club** | Manufactured nostalgia, cultural stasis, an environment stuck in one era | A preserved period can be a prison maintained by comfort rather than force. | A Machine preservation zone refuses historical change because every resident repeatedly votes for the remembered decade. | `M-17`; `MO-10` |
| 6. **My Big Flat Earth Wedding** | Flat-Earth subculture, information bubbles, elite luxury networks | Belief communities can be manipulated without every believer sharing one motive or evidence standard. | A settlement rejects the star map because its local sky is a designed enclosure and its “wrong” cosmology is operationally useful. | `M-02`; `MO-57` |
| 7. **Ghost Protocol** | Espionage archetypes, compartmentation, personal relationships across agencies | People inside secret systems are not interchangeable with the institution employing them. | A Federation liaison and Human counterintelligence officer must authenticate one another without exposing their networks. | `M-20`; `MO-18` |
| 8. **Buzzkill** | Moon-landing conspiracy, hidden lunar settlement, double identities | A false public narrative can coexist with a real event; correcting one lie does not validate every alternative story. | Apollo remains historical fact, while a later lunar relay and substitute public persona create a separate archive fork. | `M-33`; `MO-61` |
| 9. **Mole Hunt** | Insider threats, false accusation, security paranoia | Secrecy creates incentives to discover a traitor even when the evidence describes a systems failure. | A provenance breach is misread as a Xeno mole, while the actual cause is a Machine archive collision. | `M-02`, `M-20`; `MO-45` |
| 10. **Inside Reagan** | Memory forensics, childhood manipulation, password recovery | A memory can be evidence, injury, credential, and editable infrastructure at the same time. | A commander must recover a gate key from a childhood memory without accepting the memory editor’s interpretation of it. | `M-09`, `M-29`; `MO-11` |
| 11. **How Reagan Got Her Grove Back** | Secret-society summit, elite rivalry, informal governance | The hidden world is more plausible as overlapping institutions that bargain, compete, and misunderstand one another than as one flawless cabal. | The Six Hidden Tables convene; each believes the other five are subordinate and none controls the agenda. | `M-02`, `M-35`; `MO-21` |
| 12. **Whoas-Feratu** | Vampiric immortality, celebrity power, blood economy | Longevity elites generate succession problems, resource dependencies, and public mythologies around ordinary predation. | An immortal entertainment syndicate uses legal identity rotation rather than magic to survive, until one member begins harvesting noetic vitality. | `M-28`, `M-35`; `MO-26` |
| 13. **Reagan & Mychelle's Hive School Reunion** | Hive minds, psychoactive origin myths, status performance | Collective consciousness still contains embarrassment, hierarchy, memory politics, and competing personal accounts. | A social-memory complex disputes whether an embarrassing founding vision was revelation, intoxication, or both. | `M-07`, `M-30`; `MO-32` |
| 14. **We Found Love in a Popeless Place** | Religious institutions, doctrine management, political theology | Controlling an institution is not the same as controlling the transcendent experiences interpreted through it. | An Archonic identity-spoof operation changes institutional doctrine while leaving the underlying revelation unresolved. | `M-28`, `M-31`; `MO-31` |
| 15. **Brettwork** | Media capture, political puppetry, audience engineering | A manufactured leader still depends on audiences, incentives, networks, and the performer’s capacity to deviate. | A Pirate broadcaster becomes the public face of a Human bloc and discovers its audience is a noetic targeting array. | `M-02`, `M-29`; `MO-59` |
| 16. **Rontagion** | Illuminati labor, mind wiping, inter-cabal defection | Secret institutions have employees, burnout, ethics disputes, and portability problems when a worker defects. | A memory-erasure specialist seeks asylum carrying techniques that every faction considers both abusive and indispensable. | `M-02`, `M-29`, `M-35`; `MO-41` |
| 17. **Project Reboot** | Mandela effect, alternate histories, reality editing | Timeline changes are most legible when treated as branch operations that leave archive conflicts and personal costs. | A Chronology Court discovers that a private family intervention has forked an entire campaign save state. | `M-17`; `MO-01` |
| 18. **Appleton** | Hidden board governance, catastrophe justified as protection, utilitarian population management | The most dangerous secret ruler sincerely believes secrecy and engineered crisis are forms of stewardship. | A council offers the player total archive access in exchange for inheriting responsibility for Population Ledger interventions. | `M-18`, `M-35`; `MO-24` |


## What this corpus contributes that a conspiracy list does not

### Institutional ecology

The show repeatedly treats hidden organizations as employers and contractors rather than disembodied evil. In the 2099 canon, this becomes the **Six Hidden Tables**: overlapping institutions that exchange services, compete for jurisdiction, inherit old obligations, and often misread one another.

### Maintenance as plot

Reality editors, memory systems, artificial leaders, hidden colonies, and biological elites all generate upkeep. Maintenance creates workers, records, supply chains, vulnerabilities, whistleblowers, procurement scandals, and accidents. This is the most transferable design insight for commanders, towers, planets, and campaign events.

### Sincere paternalism

The final governance motif is especially relevant to the Federation and Population Ledger. A hidden council may sincerely believe it protects civilization. Sincerity does not create consent, and competence does not create legitimate authority.

### Branch-history consequences

The reality-editing premise is strongest when translated into the established four-layer time model:

- **Retrocognition** reconstructs what happened.
- **Information backflow** moves data without moving bodies.
- **Branch selection** produces competing histories and archive forks.
- **Physical chronology transit** is rare, expensive, and identity-destabilizing.

This keeps alternate-history storytelling compatible with saves, evidence chains, and multiplayer determinism.

# Mythos Integration Framework

This document translates the 2026 Alien and Space Mythos Atlas into game-facing
content without replacing the five-faction structure or changing current mechanics.

## Binding principles

1. The five factions remain Humans, Federation of Light, Xenos, Pirates, and Machines.
2. Myth systems are institutions, technologies, subcultures, historical layers, and
   mission premises - never additional top-level factions.
3. A real-world source receives its evidence label before any fictional adaptation.
4. The same visual archetype can be used by multiple operators.
5. No real ethnic, religious, national, medical, financial, or political population
   is secretly an alien species in canon.
6. A source-inspired plot may preserve one operational detail while its public story
   remains false or contaminated.
7. Satirical media such as *Inside Job* is a design corpus, not a factual source.

## New content families

### Character families

- **Chronology Court officers:** judges, archivists, branch witnesses, and temporal refugees.
- **Serpent Court continuities:** adaptive-body diplomats, legal successors, and defectors.
- **Nine-Key custodians:** Federation jurists and Machine protocol interpreters.
- **Continuity Clinic specialists:** memory doctors, identity advocates, and fraud examiners.
- **Open Archive mythographers:** Humans who trace how stories mutate through documents and media.
- **Atmospheric jurisdiction crews:** weather scientists, cloud-seeding operators, and Old Weather negotiators.
- **Anomaly-station personnel:** scientists defending sensor custody at recurrent-light and Cross-Layer Fault sites.
- **Conspiracy maintenance workers:** ordinary technicians who keep inherited secrecy systems running.
- **Ontology Cell analysts:** teams who share observations but disagree whether the operator is extraterrestrial, cryptoterrestrial, noetic, Machine, future-Human, deceptive, or theological.
- **Synthetic-revelation engineers:** experts in projection, drones, augmented reality, broadcast compromise, and noetic identity spoofing.
- **Noetic reconnaissance teams:** carefully blinded observers, statisticians, tasking officers, and provenance auditors.
- **Secret-space veterans:** people whose service records, memories, and chronology proofs disagree.
- **Warning-form interpreters:** structural engineers, folklorists, and Lattice technicians who must separate omen, cause, and retrospective association.

### Planet and battlefield families

- **Polar Null Site:** ice, buried relay, contradictory postwar archives.
- **Route Shadow:** official map and actual transit network disagree.
- **Cross-Layer Fault:** geology, noetics, sensors, and actors produce different observations.
- **Coherence Reservoir:** sacred/technical site whose energy depends on consent history.
- **Ancestor Vault:** conscious historical simulation with disputed citizenship.
- **Population Ledger World:** demographic optimization built into infrastructure.
- **Luminous Valley:** recurring lights monitored by neutral instruments.
- **Tissue Survey Zone:** ecological and genetic sampling with mixed causes.
- **Temporal Fork World:** records, memories, and terrain belong to adjacent histories.
- **Deep Jurisdiction:** cryptoterrestrial habitat intertwined with Old Weather systems.
- **Revelation Stage:** dense city where every screen, drone, headset, and noetic receiver can be synchronized into a local false contact.
- **Solar Warden Relay:** a distributed sensor and catalog node later mistaken for an off-world fleet headquarters.
- **Collider Key Site:** accelerator complex that accidentally repeats a dormant Lattice handshake while prank footage poisons public trust.
- **Consensus Retreat:** formally powerless private forum whose access network changes policy without issuing orders.
- **Warning Zone:** recurrent manifestations cluster around infrastructure already near failure.

### Dialogue variables

Future dialogue generation should be able to test:

- `evidence_class`
- `source_tradition`
- `public_explanation`
- `classified_explanation`
- `actual_operator`
- `timeline_branch`
- `identity_continuity`
- `consent_quality`
- `population_policy`
- `archive_version`
- `media_integrity`
- `religious_layer`
- `scapegoat_risk`
- `ontology_model`
- `program_maturity`
- `memory_provenance`
- `chronology_proof`
- `synthetic_revelation_channel`
- `private_network_influence`
- `warning_causality`

## Commander retrofit opportunities

### Humanity

- **Vanta:** becomes the leading myth-genealogy and archive-version specialist.
- **Nyx:** temporal and reality-editing technology tempts her redline philosophy.
- **Orin:** investigates weather and ionospheric technology stripped of conspiracy mythology.
- **Vess:** confronts Continuity Directorate emergency population controls.
- **Korrin:** discovers hidden programs through procurement and logistics rather than leaked confession.

### Federation

- **Seraph:** defends the difference between consensual coherence and devotional extraction.
- **Halder:** sees the Mercy Census as proof that protection must sometimes override refusal.
- **Lumen:** authenticates Ashtar claims, Nine Keys, and counterfeit revelation.
- **Mira:** investigates continuity clinics and postmortem consent.
- **Aurel:** oversees evacuation protocols vulnerable to cultic and Xeno imitation.

### Xenos

- **Mawlord:** seeks Causal Return Grid access to stabilize Severed identity.
- **Sevra:** is a natural Serpent Court legal-continuity operator.
- **Dregg:** turns Population Ledger optimization into military policy.
- **Ulgrim:** treats biological sampling as asset acquisition.
- **Nym:** weaponizes uncertainty by surrounding real operations with absurd versions.

### Pirates

- **Rake:** uses Route Shadows and mythic geography to evade every authority.
- **Scarlet:** treats timeline uncertainty as permission to act before evidence settles.
- **Grist:** makes useful hardware from discredited projects and hoax props.
- **Cinder:** exploits weather-control panic to conceal ordinary raids.
- **Vex:** trades identities, archive versions, and one-use backflow keys.

### Machines

- **Axiom:** argues that conscious simulations and branch copies possess personhood.
- **Nyx-R:** optimizes branch selection but rejects physical time travel as wasteful.
- **Lumen-R:** learns from identity-spoofing and Men-in-Black cleanup patterns.
- **Mawlord-R:** treats conspiracy corpora as training data and risks overfitting folklore.
- **Dregg-R:** audits the Continuity Calculus and discovers that optimization objectives are political.

## Asset direction

### Reptilian and serpent imagery

- Never make all reptiloids purple/evil or all Xenos reptilian.
- Serpent Court design should emphasize legal continuity: seals, layered masks, body records,
  inherited insignia, and multiple versions of one face.
- Federation and Pirate reptiloids must be visually normal members of those factions.

### Time and reality imagery

- Avoid generic clocks everywhere.
- Use conflicting timestamps, checksum halos, archive overlays, duplicate shadows, and
  geometry that almost aligns.
- Physical chrononauts should show severe containment and identity-stabilization equipment.

### Depopulation and population-policy imagery

- Avoid real political symbols and real demographic scapegoats.
- Use abstract ledgers, habitat-capacity maps, reproductive licenses, evacuation queues,
  and contradictory optimization targets.
- The threat should be the system and decision structure, not a racialized population.

### Mind control and noetic reconnaissance

- Real historical behavioral-abuse imagery should use institutional records, clinical rooms, consent gaps, and fragmented custody rather than supernatural hypnosis spirals.
- Noetic reconnaissance should show target blinding, competing taskers, confidence ranges, and false-positive risk.
- Project Monarch remains a later mutation, not a blanket label for all documented abuse.

### Secret space and lunar imagery

- Apollo hardware and exploration remain real in the setting. Later secret infrastructure must look chronologically and institutionally separate.
- Solar Warden should begin as catalogs, sensor relays, route plots, and duty rosters before any fleet image appears.
- Twenty-and-back characters need conflicting age markers, archived absence, and continuity equipment rather than generic supersoldier armor.

### Simulated revelation and elite-network imagery

- Blue Beam-style operations are local multisystem deception: screens, drones, AR, projection, compromised broadcasts, and noetic overlays.
- Private forums should look like access and consensus networks, not occult caricatures.
- Ontology Cells should use the same evidence wall with different explanatory overlays.

### Inside Job-inspired tone

- Secret offices should look worked-in, underfunded, inherited, and bureaucratic.
- Impossible systems need maintenance panels, forms, temporary fixes, and exhausted staff.
- Comedy can appear in logs and dialogue, but primary faction tragedy remains sincere.

## Implementation order

1. Add the Mythos Atlas and this framework to the lore wiki only.
2. Add Mythos Operation IDs to a data registry without loading them into the game.
3. Retrofit commander dossier text and relationships.
4. Add world tags and scenario hooks to deterministic planet dossiers.
5. Add dialogue variables and small pre-battle exchanges.
6. Commission new portraits, tower plates, and world art only after copy locks.
7. Implement any new mechanic in a separate balance-reviewed change.

## No-mechanics promise

This expansion does not itself change tower damage, costs, unit stats, campaign
randomness, saves, network state, scenario selection, or world generation. It supplies
lore, content tags, mission concepts, and future integration contracts.

# Mythos Operations

This registry converts the source-genealogy work in the [Alien and Space Mythos Atlas](../research/alien-space-mythos-atlas-2026.md) into **74 optional campaign, Archive War, dialogue, and world-dossier premises**. These are fiction hooks, not statements that the source conspiracies are true.

## Use rules

1. Keep the five factions unchanged.
2. Retain the source evidence class in any dossier that invokes a real tradition.
3. Never map a real ethnic, religious, national, medical, or political population to an alien bloodline.
4. Preserve one conventional and one speculative reading until a mission intentionally resolves the question.
5. Mythos Operations do not alter mechanics until a separate implementation and balance pass.

# Compiled 0.4.0 Mythos Connection Modules

# Alien and Space Mythos Connection Delta - August 2026

Release **0.4.0** expands the lore from 37 to **60 myth systems**, from 74 to **100 Mythos Operations**, and adds **28 global case dossiers**, **57 documented mutation edges**, **12 new cross-faction systems**, and an 18-episode *Inside Job* design map.

## Most important conclusions

1. **Mega-conspiracy is a merger.** Reptilian rulers, underground bases, crash retrieval, population reduction, time travel, secret space, prison planets, and staged contact entered the culture through different decades and source types.
2. **Real abuse creates Trust Debt, not unlimited proof.** MKULTRA, coercive sterilization, covert weather modification, black aerospace, and information operations make skepticism understandable; they do not validate every later extension.
3. **Inside Job contributes institutional design, not evidence.** Its useful lesson is that hidden power has budgets, layoffs, maintenance, succession, interpersonal conflict, and error.
4. **Global cases need a Translation Horizon.** Archive access and cultural reach determine which incidents become canonical far more than evidence strength alone.
5. **Time travel must be tiered.** Retrocognition, information backflow, branch selection, and physical transit have different proof burdens and campaign consequences.
6. **Population governance requires a ladder.** Public demographic planning, voluntary incentives, coercive control, selection, and extermination cannot be treated as one category.
7. **No real population becomes an alien proxy.** Antisemitic, racist, anti-migrant, and medical scapegoating mutations are documented only to exclude them from canon.

## New maintained modules

- [Myth Mutation Graph](myth-mutation-graph.md)
- [Global UFO and Anomaly Case Atlas](global-ufo-case-atlas.md)
