# Myth Mutation Graph

This graph records how motifs moved between fiction, religion, contactee movements, official secrecy, documented abuse, scientific speculation, media, and online conspiracism. An edge means **historical or interpretive influence**, not that both nodes describe one real actor.

## Six mechanisms that connect the traditions

1. **Archive compression:** separate files, dates, and agencies become one story because they share a label.
2. **Credibility kernels:** a real secret program or abuse makes a much larger unsupported claim feel plausible.
3. **Morphology capture:** one influential image changes how later witnesses, artists, hoaxers, and diplomats describe bodies.
4. **Theology translation:** angels, demons, ascended masters, aliens, simulations, and Machine interfaces are mapped onto one another without proving identity.
5. **Platform convergence:** books, radio, television, conventions, forums, and algorithmic feeds merge traditions that originally had no relationship.
6. **Political totalization:** many institutions and incentives are compressed into one all-powerful cabal.

## Canon rule

A false story may preserve one displaced operational detail. The detail does not authenticate the whole story. A genuine program may explain why a myth formed without explaining every sighting attached to it. A sincere witness may be wrong about ontology without being dishonest.

# Global UFO and Anomaly Case Atlas

This atlas prevents the setting from becoming a U.S.-only mythology and prevents a famous case name from collapsing every witness, sensor, document, and later interpretation into one evidentiary object.

## Case ledger

| ID | Case | Date | Location | Evidence | Supported core | Does not establish | Canon use |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `CASE-001` | **Kenneth Arnold sighting** | 1947-06-24 | United States | C/B | “Flying saucer” terminology emerged from reporting around Arnold’s description, shaping later perception and language. | Language can standardize morphology before evidence does. | Human archive tutorial |
| `CASE-002` | **Roswell** | 1947-07 | United States | A/B/D/E | A documented recovery of unusual balloon material became the center of crash-retrieval mythology. | Contemporaneous, official-later, witness, and forged layers must remain separate. | MO-75 / MO-76 |
| `CASE-003` | **Washington National Airport wave** | 1952-07 | United States | B/C | Multiple nights of radar and visual reports generated national attention and competing weather/radar explanations. | A case label can compress several tracks and nights. | Air-defense overload arc |

| `CASE-005` | **Socorro / Lonnie Zamora** | 1964-04-24 | United States | B/C | A law-enforcement witness reported an object and occupants; the case retains strong witness provenance but no confirmed origin. | Good witness provenance does not settle ontology. | Human residual case |
| `CASE-006` | **Westall** | 1966-04-06 | Australia | C/B | Many students and staff reported objects; surviving documentation is incomplete. | Long-term witness consistency and missing records are separate variables. | MO-82 |
| `CASE-007` | **Shag Harbour** | 1967-10-04 | Canada | B/C | Lights were reported descending toward water and a search followed. | Search activity does not establish recovered craft. | MO-86 |

| `CASE-019` | **Aguadilla** | 2013-04-26 | Puerto Rico | A/B | AARO found no anomalous or transmedium behavior and offered a conventional interpretation. | Visual strangeness is not transmedium proof. | False-transmedium tutorial |

| `CASE-028` | **Area 51 U-2/OXCART sightings** | 1950s-1960s | United States | A | Secret high-altitude aircraft accounted for many reports and show how classified technology creates durable anomaly ecosystems. | Not every report near a secret site is explained by the acknowledged aircraft. | Black-aerospace baseline |

## Global archive rules

- A case has an **incident date**, **record date**, **release date**, **translation date**, and **local-ingestion date**.
- The most famous case is not automatically the strongest case. Searchability, language, media access, and cultural templates shape fame.
- A named event can contain multiple unrelated observations. Event Segmentation Doctrine runs before attribution.
- Witness sincerity, witness memory, sensor integrity, and external ontology are scored separately.
- Official investigation establishes official attention, not alien origin.

# Inside Job Design Corpus Map

## Why it belongs in the research stack

*Inside Job* is useful as a **fictional design corpus**, not evidence. Its most valuable move is to treat global conspiracies as workplace systems staffed by people with budgets, rivalries, emotions, maintenance burdens, and career incentives. That is the opposite of an omniscient cabal.

Only episode titles and high-level design observations are used here. Scripts, dialogue, scene recreation, and proprietary characters are not copied.

## Episode-to-design map

| ID | Episode | Design domain for Cosmic Conquest |
|---|---|---|
| `IJ-01` | **Unpresidented** | Robot leaders, succession, and the difference between public office and operational control. |
| `IJ-02` | **Clone Gunman** | Cloning, assassination mythology, budget cuts, and institutional triage. |
| `IJ-03` | **Blue Bloods** | Reptoid elites, donor politics, public relations, and cross-species class power. |
| `IJ-04` | **Sex Machina** | Dating-data aggregation, synthetic intimacy, surveillance incentives, and creator responsibility. |
| `IJ-05` | **The Brettfast Club** | Memory editing, manufactured identity, and emotional evidence. |
| `IJ-06` | **My Big Flat Earth Wedding** | Competing conspiracy subcultures, social belonging, and profitable false models. |
| `IJ-07` | **Ghost Protocol** | Mole people, hidden infrastructure, territorial jurisdiction, and ritualized diplomacy. |
| `IJ-08` | **Buzzkill** | Moon-landing infrastructure, celebrity mythology, and cover-story maintenance. |
| `IJ-09` | **Mole Hunt** | Counterintelligence, leak attribution, and loyalty testing. |
| `IJ-10` | **Inside Reagan** | Memory privacy, parental manipulation, and the ethics of psychological access. |
| `IJ-11` | **How Reagan Got Her Grove Back** | Secret societies as competing institutions rather than one pyramid. |
| `IJ-12` | **Whoas-Feratu** | Immortal elites, biological continuity, cultural extraction, and obsolescence. |
| `IJ-13` | **Reagan and Myc's Hive School Reunion** | Hive minds, social-memory politics, class resentment, and collective identity. |
| `IJ-14` | **We Found Love in a Popeless Place** | Religious authority, succession, and diplomacy with mythic institutions. |
| `IJ-15` | **Brettwork** | Media influence, political identity, narrative ecosystems, and inadvertent radicalization. |
| `IJ-16` | **Rontagion** | Biosecurity, misinformation, elite immunity, and unequal emergency powers. |
| `IJ-17` | **Project Reboot** | Reality alteration, branch memory, personal motive, and catastrophic maintenance technology. |
| `IJ-18` | **Appleton** | Timeline cleanup, moral compromise, career succession, and the human cost of optimal outcomes. |

## The ten transferable principles

### Bureaucratic friction

Hidden power is divided by forms, budgets, approvals, staffing, maintenance, and turf wars.

### Budget test

Every conspiracy consumes labor, facilities, supply chains, data storage, training, and political capital.

### Jurisdiction test

Secret offices overlap, contradict, and sometimes sabotage one another because no one has universal authority.

### Succession test

A hidden system must explain who inherits command keys, memories, liabilities, and legitimacy.

### Procurement test

Impossible technology still needs materials, contractors, test failures, repair, and disposal.

### Error budget

Coverups fail through ordinary mistakes, personality conflicts, and poorly specified objectives.

### Partial truth

A false public story may protect a real but different program; the true part should not authenticate the false whole.

### Human motive

Characters need love, fear, status, guilt, ideology, grief, or ambition beyond faction doctrine.

### One-percent-better rule

A vast institution is believable when it improves outcomes marginally, not when it flawlessly controls everything.

### Comedy boundary

Satire can remix harmless or fictional motifs, but must not launder scapegoating or dehumanizing conspiracy claims into canon.

## The Cognito Principle

Before any covert institution enters canon, answer all of the following:

1. What is its mandate?
2. Who funds it?
3. Who works there and why?
4. What does it procure and repair?
5. Where does its jurisdiction end?
6. Who audits it?
7. How does succession work?
8. Which rival office wants its budget?
9. What is its ordinary error rate?
10. What happens when it fails publicly?

An organization that cannot answer those questions is a rumor, not a usable faction institution.

## Structured data

See [`data/research/inside-job-corpus.json`](../../data/research/inside-job-corpus.json).

---

# Temporal Mythology and the Time War

## Historical source lanes

Time-travel conspiracy lore is not one tradition. The **Philadelphia Experiment** entered public mythology through 1950s Allende correspondence about a supposed 1943 naval experiment. **Montauk** literature later fused military mind control, psychic work, portals, missing children, and the Philadelphia story. **Project Pegasus** added testimony about secret government time travel. The **Chronovisor** supplied a religious retrocognition device. The **Mandela Effect** and simulation discourse made timeline alteration participatory and digital. *Inside Job* supplies a fictional reality-editing machine whose most useful lesson is that personal motives and maintenance failures matter more than abstract omnipotence.

## Temporal Technology Ladder

| Level | Capability | Information cost | Canon risk |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Retrocognition**: reconstructing a probable past from surviving state | Cannot recover erased context perfectly | Mistaken certainty and synthetic evidence |
| 2 | **Information backflow**: sending a small pattern backward | Creates self-authenticating rumors and impossible provenance | Prophecy, forged treaties, archive loops |
| 3 | **Branch selection**: preserving one possible outcome while alternatives remain recorded | Requires immense noetic coherence and destroys political legitimacy | Project Reboot, Mandela-like residue, erased lives |
| 4 | **Physical chronology transit**: moving a body or durable object across time | Rare, unstable, and never a general-purpose weapon | Descendant intervention and causal jurisdiction |

## Canon architecture

The **Chronology Court** does not decide which timeline is “real.” It decides which records, persons, contracts, debts, and crimes remain legally actionable after a branch event. **Temporal Jurisdiction** prevents future descendants from treating present Humanity as property. The **Source Exposure Graph** records whether an impossible memory came from experience, prior media, backflow, simulation, or another copied mind.

## Faction motives

- **Humans:** use backflow to prevent external ownership, then fracture over whether future survival justifies present manipulation.
- **Federation:** restrict temporal contact because manufactured consent can originate in the future.
- **Xenos:** create closed causal contracts whose subjects appear to have always agreed.
- **Pirates:** smuggle branch memories, extinct identities, and route keys that exist in only one history.
- **Machines:** preserve every branch as an archive and may value deleted simulations as living populations.

## Non-negotiable rule

A corrupted photograph, altered memory, or historical inconsistency is not automatically a timeline event. Conventional editing, expectation, metadata loss, memory reconstruction, and disinformation remain live explanations until the story deliberately resolves them.

---

# Population Control and Depopulation Genealogy

## Why the genealogy must be separated

Several historical facts make population-control conspiracy narratives emotionally plausible: coercive sterilization programs existed; eugenics influenced law and medicine; states have rationed migration, reproduction, land, and public services; and private forums and public institutions discuss demographic and environmental futures. Those facts do **not** establish one continuous secret plan linking Agenda 21, the Great Reset, vaccines, climate policy, the Georgia Guidestones, aliens, and extermination.

## Population Policy Ladder

| Level | Description | Consent condition | Canon classification |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | Measurement and demographic forecasting | Public methods and contestable models | Ordinary governance |
| 1 | Public planning for housing, food, health, climate, or migration | Political accountability required | Governance conflict |
| 2 | Voluntary incentives or family-support policy | Reversible and informed choice | Ethical but contestable |
| 3 | Access restrictions, emergency triage, or migration ceilings | Coercion risk and unequal burden | High scrutiny |
| 4 | Reproductive control, forced sterilization, or compulsory selection | Consent absent | Documented atrocity category |
| 5 | Population culling, engineered famine, or extermination | No legitimate consent possible | Xeno/totalitarian crime |

## Historical mutation map

- **Documented eugenics** becomes a credibility kernel.
- **Limits-to-growth modeling** is recast as a hidden extermination blueprint.
- **Agenda 21**, a public sustainable-development plan, is reframed as covert territorial and population control.
- **Georgia Guidestones** turn an anonymous monument inscription into evidence of an operating organization.
- **Alternative 3**, an admitted mockumentary, supplies elite evacuation, abandoned populations, and secret Mars colonies.
- **Great Reset** rhetoric is compressed with all preceding material into one total plan.
- UFO and reptilian mythology then supplies a nonhuman beneficiary.

## 2099 adaptation

The **Population Ledger** is not one document. It is a family of incompatible emergency plans: ark capacity, habitat carrying limits, evacuation priority, gene-bank diversity, biosphere restoration, and Xeno yield quotas. The **Trust Debt** variable rises whenever institutions conceal real coercion; high Trust Debt makes even legitimate emergency planning politically unstable.

The Xenos’ most dangerous doctrine is not “kill most people.” It is **population optimization as ownership**: deciding which bodies, cultures, memories, and traits deserve continuity. Federation planners can reproduce the same harm through paternalistic rescue. Human governments can reproduce it through settlement law. Machines can reproduce it by preserving species while deleting persons.

## Guardrail

No present-day public health, climate, development, financial, ethnic, religious, or political group is secretly designated a Xeno proxy. Harmful scapegoating narratives are documented as contamination and excluded from canon.

---

# Shadow Government, Reptilian, and Cabal Genealogy

## The historical merger

The modern “reptilian overlord” package is a late merger. Older serpent symbolism and Theosophical lost-race ideas fed weird fiction such as Robert E. Howard’s serpent infiltrators. Postwar UFO culture added underground bases, humanoid visitors, abductions, and secret treaties. New World Order literature supplied emergency powers, private elites, finance, and world-government fears. In the 1990s, UFO and New World Order conspiracism converged visibly, and David Icke’s synthesis added a malevolent serpent race to the one-cabal model.

## Why one cabal is the wrong design

Real secrecy is distributed among agencies, contractors, legislatures, courts, private networks, security compartments, and informal influence. A single mastermind explanation deletes the most useful drama: incompatible mandates, incentives, legal interpretations, budgets, personal rivalries, procurement failures, and incomplete information.

The canon therefore replaces “the Illuminati” with **overlapping hidden tables**:

1. Human continuity and black aerospace.
2. Federation contact and quarantine.
3. Xeno concessions and client institutions.
4. Pirate route neutrality and illicit access.
5. Machine maintenance permissions.
6. Temporal contamination and archive forks.

Membership overlaps without creating one sovereign. The same intermediary may appear in several archives and create the illusion of one organization.

## Reptiloid morphology is not ideology

Reptiloid bodies are widespread and politically neutral. The **Serpent Court** is an Extraction Compact institution that maintains corporate identities across bodies, heirs, and legal shells. It contains multiple species, and reptiloid dissidents serve every other faction. Draco is an imperial polity, not a biological destiny.

## Anti-scapegoat boundary

The *Protocols of the Elders of Zion* is a documented antisemitic forgery, not a source to mine for “hidden truth.” Great Replacement narratives similarly turn demographic change into racialized secret-plot claims. Cosmic Conquest may portray fictional institutions that manipulate populations, but it must never map Jews, migrants, religious minorities, financial workers, public-health staff, or any real population onto an alien cabal.

## The Cognito Principle

A hidden institution must show its payroll, contractors, maintenance, succession, jurisdiction, oversight failures, and internal opposition. The more powerful it is, the larger its logistics and error surface must become. Apparent total control is normally **Cabal Compression Error**: researchers have mistaken multiple influence networks for one command structure.

---

# Abduction, Hybrid, and Memory Genealogy

## Source sequence

The modern abduction complex developed across several layers: the Hill encounter and hypnosis; later missing-time narratives; medical and reproductive themes; Grey and Zeta iconography; Budd Hopkins and David Jacobs-style hybrid-program interpretations; Whitley Strieber’s *Communion* and its influential visitor image; sleep-paralysis and memory-reconstruction research; and later future-Human explanations.

These are not interchangeable evidence categories. A reported experience may involve an external event, sleep paralysis, medical trauma, suggestion, dissociation, dream material, deliberate staging, noetic contact, or several layers at once. Hypnosis can increase confidence without guaranteeing accuracy. A familiar image can alter subsequent memory even when the witness is sincere.

## Four separate questions

1. **Did the person experience something unusual?**
2. **What was present externally?**
3. **How was the memory formed, retrieved, and changed?**
4. **Who benefits from the interpretation?**

The setting never answers question two merely because question one is yes.

## Source Exposure Graph

Every witness record stores exposure to prior books, films, interviews, group discussion, investigator prompts, online communities, official briefings, altered memories, and synthetic media. Similarity after exposure has a different evidentiary meaning from similarity in sealed first accounts.

## Hybrid programs in canon

There is no one universal hybrid program. Several distinct systems exist:

- Xeno client-body engineering and reproductive dependency.
- Federation medical adaptation performed under disputed emergency consent.
- Human gene-clade settlement programs.
- Pirate identity and body markets.
- Machine reconstruction of persons from partial biological and memory archives.
- Future-Human attempts to preserve descendant probabilities.

“Hybrid” therefore describes a relationship among ancestry, body, memory, and law—not a moral category.

## Witness Interview Integrity Protocol

- Record individual first accounts before group discussion.
- Preserve exact questions and interviewer behavior.
- Timestamp drawings and confidence changes.
- Separate free recall from prompted recall.
- Record prior cultural exposure.
- Preserve model version and prompt history for AI interviewers.
- Never convert witness distress or sincerity into automatic external attribution.
- Never treat uncertain bodies or reproductive material as ownerless evidence.

## Gameplay use

Abduction missions should reward careful interview order, medical ethics, source separation, and relationship-building. The strongest reveal is often not “the witness lied” or “the aliens were real,” but that several actors manipulated different layers of one genuine experience.

---

# Moon, Mars, and Secret Space Mythology

## The source families

Moon and Mars conspiracy lore merges several unrelated traditions:

- Apollo-denial claims despite surviving hardware, imagery, samples, tracking, and laser-ranging evidence.
- Lunar-anomaly and artificial-Moon theories.
- Black Knight and ancient-satellite folklore.
- Phobos-as-artifact speculation.
- Cydonia’s low-resolution “Face” and later natural-landform imaging.
- Area 51 and genuine black aerospace.
- Roswell retrieval mythology and S-4 claims.
- *Alternative 3*, a 1977 mockumentary about elite evacuation to Mars.
- Solar Warden, twenty-and-back, and breakaway-civilization narratives.

The game keeps each genealogy visible.

## Factual anchors

Apollo missions occurred. Area 51 tested advanced reconnaissance aircraft. Cydonia’s face-like image is explained by terrain, resolution, and illumination. Phobos is a natural irregular moon under ongoing study. Roswell has an official classified-balloon explanation. None of these facts prevents later fictional infrastructure from being built on the Moon, Mars, or Phobos; it prevents the fiction from claiming the older myths were therefore historically correct.

## The secret-space credibility kernel

Classified aerospace can create real unusual sightings, worker secrecy, cover stories, restricted airspace, compartmented contractors, missing public records, and technology gaps. It cannot by itself sustain an invisible interplanetary civilization without launches, logistics, power, life support, supply chains, casualties, communications, and succession.

## Proposal-Reality Gap

Every secret-space claim must be classified as one of:

1. Idea or scenario.
2. Briefing or concept art.
3. Proposed program.
4. Approved compartment.
5. Funded and staffed operation.
6. Operation with hardware.
7. Operation with offworld presence.
8. Demonstrated strategic result.

A dramatic name cannot skip the ladder.

## 2099 synthesis

- **Apollo Omission:** Apollo was real; a later unrelated relay was redacted and contaminated trust in the entire record.
- **Cydonia Redirect:** the Face is natural and deliberately exploited as an attention sink.
- **S-4 Compartment:** a real future laboratory reuses a legendary label to poison leaks.
- **Alternative Three:** an actual evacuation fleet adopts the mockumentary’s name, turning disbelief into security.
- **Solar Warden:** first a sensor-fusion network, later a small real patrol service whose name is mistaken for proof of the older fleet legend.
- **Phobos Installation:** the moon is natural; the installation is younger than the moon and therefore does not require an artificial satellite theory.

## Campaign stakes

The core conflict is not whether hidden space programs are exciting. It is who was excluded from the first offworld institutions, who owns the infrastructure built under secrecy, and whether a breakaway civilization can claim independence from the people who unknowingly financed it.

---

# Shadow Bureaucracy Design Principles

This module turns the strongest structural lessons from *Inside Job*, archive history, and conspiracy scholarship into original Cosmic Conquest design rules.

## The Cognito Checklist

Every covert institution needs:

| Question | Required answer |
|---|---|
| Mandate | What problem was it legally or practically created to solve? |
| Budget | Who pays, in what currency, and who can cut funding? |
| Staff | Who performs ordinary work and what do they want? |
| Procurement | Where do rare materials, bodies, software, and replacement parts come from? |
| Jurisdiction | Which world, population, medium, or timeline can it lawfully affect? |
| Oversight | Who can investigate, audit, or dissolve it? |
| Succession | Who inherits keys, memories, debts, and legitimacy? |
| Rivalry | Which office wants the same mission or budget? |
| Error rate | What mundane mistakes does it make? |
| Public interface | What cover organization or service touches ordinary life? |
| Failure state | What happens when it becomes visible or loses control? |

## Design prohibitions

- No omniscient cabal that flawlessly controls every faction.
- No “classified” label used as proof of the claimant’s preferred story.
- No real ethnic or religious group converted into an alien lineage.
- No conspiracy that lacks logistics, labor, maintenance, or institutional conflict.
- No timeline rewrite that erases its victims from moral accounting.

## Trust Debt

Trust Debt is generated by documented abuses, false denials, forged evidence, uncorrected archive errors, coercive emergency policy, and secret programs that later become public. High Trust Debt increases resistance to genuine warnings and makes counterfeit revelations cheaper. It does not raise the factual probability of unrelated claims.

## Gameplay hooks

- Budget votes can weaken a secret office without revealing it.
- Procurement raids expose technology provenance.
- Jurisdiction disputes create three-way battles without a villain faction.
- Succession crises generate multiple authentic command keys.
- Audit missions may prove misconduct without proving aliens.
- A cover story can be false while the concealed program is mundane.
- Rival offices can each possess one correct fragment and one damaging assumption.

## Character rule

Every commander needs a personal motive that survives if their faction doctrine is removed. Love, shame, grief, status, fear, ambition, guilt, curiosity, or loyalty should determine how they use institutional power.

---

| `MO-91` | **Wow Repeat** | Technosignature candidates | A narrowband signal repeats only when a Pirate translation graph and a Machine clock are combined, making both owners indispensable. |
| `MO-92` | **BLC1 Candidate** | Technosignature candidates | A signal is correctly resolved as local interference, but the interference source is a clandestine Human transmitter monitoring the same star. |
| `MO-93` | **Remains Protocol** | Specimen ethics | A supposed alien specimen is probably human, but several factions have already copied, patented, and weaponized its genome. |
| `MO-94` | **Piri Reis Overlay** | Mythic geography | A false Antarctic interpretation conceals a modern route key inserted into a famous historical map by a later intelligence service. |
| `MO-95` | **Pole Shift Countdown** | Cataclysm mythology | Magnetic reversal, climate migration, orbital forcing, and Lattice coordinate drift are fused into one apocalyptic deadline. |
| `MO-96` | **Treaty at the Airfield** | Contact and temporal information | A forged Eisenhower treaty uses a valid Accord syntax that should not exist for another century. |
| `MO-97` | **Cydonia Redirect** | Moon Mars and secret space | The Face is natural and functions as an intentional distraction from a dull-looking site that is genuinely artificial. |
| `MO-98` | **Descendant Claim** | Temporal politics | Future Human clades demand authority over present decisions because erasing their probability is, to them, genocide. |
| `MO-99` | **Synthetic Witness** | AI memory and simulation | A copied mind remembers an encounter sincerely, but forensic analysis shows the event occurred only in a deleted simulation branch. |
| `MO-100` | **Translation Horizon** | Global archive asymmetry | A regional case changes galactic politics only after its archive is translated; the translation model may have merged two species names. |

## Implementation rule

Each operation should expose at least two plausible interpretations until its final decision point. A mission may resolve one evidentiary layer while leaving another open.
